// How much food a pet gets for its weight. The weigh step shows this card, and the food step will
// ask the player to measure this much in the measuring cup (lines at 1/4, 1/2, 3/4 and 1 cup).
const FOOD = {
  dog: [ { upTo: 10, cups: .25, label: 'up to 10 lb' }, { upTo: 20, cups: .5, label: '11 to 20 lb' },
         { upTo: 40, cups: .75, label: '21 to 40 lb' }, { upTo: Infinity, cups: 1, label: 'over 40 lb' } ],
  cat: [ { upTo: 10, cups: .25, label: 'up to 10 lb' }, { upTo: Infinity, cups: .5, label: 'over 10 lb' } ],
};
const CUPS = { .25: '¼ cup', .5: '½ cup', .75: '¾ cup', 1: '1 cup' };
function foodFor(p, k) { return FOOD[k].find(r => p.weight <= r.upTo); }

  // the crates: which two dogs and which two cats are there changes every visit (see CRATES below)
  catSlots: [ { cx: 372, bottom: 428, w: 140, bedFront: 'M298 417 Q372 431 446 417 L446 442 L298 442 Z' },     // the pink cushion

// Who is in the crates this visit: the town picks when you walk in and sends it along (#cat&crates=bella,spud,luna,felix: two dogs, then two cats);
// opening the game on its own picks at random. Every dog can sit in the bed; every cat with a sleeping picture can nap on a cushion.
const CRATES = (() => {
  const m = location.hash.match(/crates=([\w,]+)/), want = m ? m[1].split(',') : [];
  const dogs = PETS.dog, cats = PETS.cat.filter(c => ART[c.id + 'Sleep']), find = (list, id) => list.find(p => p.id === id);
  const shuffled = l => l.map(v => [Math.random(), v]).sort((a, b) => a[0] - b[0]).map(v => v[1]);
  let twoDogs = [find(dogs, want[0]), find(dogs, want[1])].filter(Boolean);
  if (twoDogs.length < 2 || twoDogs[0] === twoDogs[1]) twoDogs = shuffled(dogs).slice(0, 2);
  let two = [find(cats, want[2]), find(cats, want[3])].filter(Boolean);
  if (two.length < 2 || two[0] === two[1]) two = shuffled(cats).slice(0, 2);
  return { dogs: twoDogs, cats: two };
})();
function crateDogH(d) {   // bigger dogs sit taller in the bed (the same size groups as the food chart)
  const k = SIZES[Math.max(0, FOOD.dog.findIndex(r => d.weight <= r.upTo))] || 1;
  return EXTRAS.dogSlots[0].h * k / SIZES[1] * (d.weight <= 20 ? .9 : 1);   // small dogs a little smaller, so they don't look as big as the big dogs
}
function extrasSvg() {
  let s = '';
  EXTRAS.dogSlots.forEach((p, i) => { const d = CRATES.dogs[i]; if (!d) return; const h = crateDogH(d), w = h * 443 / 560;
    s += `<g id="crate-${d.id}" class="extra"><image href="${ART[d.art]}" x="${p.cx - w / 2}" y="${p.bottom - h}" width="${w}" height="${h}"/>
    <clipPath id="bedFrontClip-${i}"><path d="${p.bedFront}"/></clipPath><image href="${ART.room}" x="0" y="0" width="1984" height="1116" clip-path="url(#bedFrontClip-${i})"/></g>`; });
  EXTRAS.catSlots.forEach((c, i) => { const cat = CRATES.cats[i]; if (!cat) return; const ch = c.w * 400 / 498;
    s += `<g id="crate-${cat.id}" class="extra"><image href="${ART[cat.id + 'Sleep']}" x="${c.cx - c.w / 2}" y="${c.bottom - ch}" width="${c.w}" height="${ch}"/>
    <clipPath id="catBedClip-${i}"><path d="${c.bedFront}"/></clipPath><image href="${ART.room}" x="0" y="0" width="1984" height="1116" clip-path="url(#catBedClip-${i})"/></g>`; });
  EXTRAS.bowls.forEach(b => { const h = b.w * 236 / 360;
    s += `<image id="${b.id}" class="extra" href="${ART[b.art]}" x="${b.cx - b.w / 2}" y="${b.bottom - h}" width="${b.w}" height="${h}"/>`; });
  return s;
}
const hideExtra = id => { const e = document.getElementById(id); if (e) e.style.opacity = 0; };
const RUG = { cx: 992, bottom: 965, h: 400 };   // h is a big dog's height; smaller dogs are drawn smaller
const SIZES = [.72, .82, .92, 1];   // up to 10 lb, 11 to 20, 21 to 40, over 40 (the food chart's groups)
const CAT_K = .72;   // every cat is drawn the same size
function sizeK(p) { if (kind === 'cat') return CAT_K; const rules = FOOD[kind] || FOOD.dog; return SIZES[Math.max(0, rules.findIndex(r => p.weight <= r.upTo))] || 1; }
const rugH = () => RUG.h * sizeK(pet), eatH = () => EAT.h * sizeK(pet), tubH = () => TUB_DOG.h * sizeK(pet);          // where the pet sits on the rug
const ON_SCALE = { cx: 1322, bottom: 726, k: .63 };     // where it sits on the scale, smaller (it is farther back)

let task = 0, pet = null, kind = 'dog', page = 0, busy = false;
const room = document.getElementById('room');

const PAW_SHAPE = col => `<ellipse cx="39" cy="18" rx="10" ry="14" fill="${col}"/><ellipse cx="62" cy="18" rx="10" ry="14" fill="${col}"/><ellipse cx="18" cy="42" rx="9" ry="13" fill="${col}" transform="rotate(-20 18 42)"/><ellipse cx="82" cy="42" rx="9" ry="13" fill="${col}" transform="rotate(20 82 42)"/><path d="M50 44 C32 44 22 66 24 78 C26 88 38 86 50 84 C62 86 74 88 76 78 C78 66 68 44 50 44 Z" fill="${col}"/>`;   // the same paw as the boy/girl card
const ICONS = {
  paw: `<svg x="-22" y="-20" width="44" height="41" viewBox="0 0 100 92">${PAW_SHAPE('#8a5a3b')}</svg>`,
  scale: `<rect x="-20" y="2" width="40" height="14" rx="5" fill="#8f98a3"/><rect x="-17" y="-1" width="34" height="8" rx="4" fill="#b7c0ca"/><rect x="-7" y="1" width="14" height="5" rx="2" fill="#dff3ff"/>`,
  bowl: `<image href="${ART.bowlFood}" x="-22" y="-14.5" width="44" height="29"/>`,   // the room's own food bowl picture (Mac, Nov 26, 2026)
  bubbles: `<circle cx="-8" cy="6" r="11" fill="#bfe3fb"/><circle cx="10" cy="-4" r="9" fill="#d7eefc"/><circle cx="4" cy="14" r="6" fill="#a9d8f8"/><circle cx="-11" cy="2" r="3" fill="#fff"/>`,
  drop: `<path d="M0 -18 C8 -6 14 2 14 8 A14 14 0 0 1 -14 8 C-14 2 -8 -6 0 -18Z" fill="#8fd0f5"/><ellipse cx="-5" cy="6" rx="3" ry="5" fill="#fff" opacity=".7"/>`,
  towel: `<rect x="-20" y="-12" width="40" height="26" rx="10" fill="#9fdcbc"/><rect x="-20" y="-12" width="40" height="10" rx="5" fill="#bae5ca"/>`,
  brush: `<rect x="-4" y="-2" width="26" height="9" rx="4.5" fill="#d8a067" transform="rotate(-30)"/><ellipse cx="-9" cy="6" rx="13" ry="9" fill="#c98a5a"/><ellipse cx="-9" cy="3" rx="10" ry="6" fill="#f2d3a8"/>`,
  feather: `<path d="M-16 18 L6 -4" stroke="#d8a067" stroke-width="5" stroke-linecap="round"/><path d="M6 -4 C 0 -18, 10 -24, 20 -22 C 22 -10, 16 -2, 6 -4 Z" fill="#e0457b"/><path d="M8 -6 C 12 -13, 15 -17, 19 -20" stroke="#f7b6cc" stroke-width="2.5" fill="none" stroke-linecap="round"/>`,
  clip: `<path d="M-4 0 L20 -10" stroke="#f28fb0" stroke-width="8" stroke-linecap="round"/><path d="M-4 4 L20 14" stroke="#e0457b" stroke-width="8" stroke-linecap="round"/><path d="M-20 -1 Q-24 2 -20 5 L-2 5 L-2 -1 Z" fill="#b7c0ca"/><circle cx="-2" cy="2" r="4.5" fill="#9aa6b2"/>`,
  check: `<circle r="19" fill="#5fae7a"/><path d="M-9 0 L-2 7 L10 -7" fill="none" stroke="#fff" stroke-width="5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>`,
};
const icon = k => `<svg viewBox="-24 -24 48 48">${ICONS[k]}</svg>`;
const say = text => text.replace('your pet', pet ? pet.name : 'your pet');

function drawTasks(popCur) {
  document.getElementById('tasks').innerHTML = TASKS.map((t, i) => {
    const done = i < task, cur = i === task;
    const words = t.name;   // tasks are actions (Weigh), even when done
    return (i ? `<i class="trail${done || cur ? ' done' : ''}"></i>` : '') + `<div class="task${cur ? ' cur' : ''}${done ? ' done' : ''}${cur && popCur ? ' pop' : ''}">${icon(done ? 'check' : t.icon)}<span>${words}</span></div>`;
  }).join('');
}

function tagSvg(t) {
  const w = Math.max(120, t.name.length * 20 + 44), cx = t.x + t.w / 2;
  const y = t.tag === 'top' ? t.y - 58 : t.y + t.h - 6;
  if (t.name === 'Cats' || t.name === 'Dogs') return `<g class="tag pick-btn"><rect x="${cx - w / 2 - 8}" y="${y - 4}" width="${w + 16}" height="60" rx="18" fill="#2f2540" transform="translate(0 7)"/>
    <rect x="${cx - w / 2 - 8}" y="${y - 4}" width="${w + 16}" height="60" rx="18" fill="#4a3b5c"/>
    <text x="${cx}" y="${y + 36}" text-anchor="middle" font-size="30" font-weight="bold" fill="#fff">${t.name}</text></g>`;
  return `<g class="tag"><rect x="${cx - w / 2}" y="${y}" width="${w}" height="48" rx="24" fill="#fff" stroke="#4a3b5c" stroke-width="4"/>
    <text x="${cx}" y="${y + 33}" text-anchor="middle" font-size="27" font-weight="bold" fill="#4a3b5c">${t.name}</text></g>`;
}
const ringSvg = (x, y, w, h) => `<rect class="ring" x="${x}" y="${y}" width="${w}" height="${h}" rx="40" fill="rgba(255,246,176,.18)" stroke="#fff6b0" stroke-width="14"/>`;

const ARROW = `<path d="M-15 -80 H15 V-42 H36 L0 0 L-36 -42 H-15 Z" transform="translate(0 7)" fill="#b8336a" stroke="#b8336a" stroke-width="12" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
  <path d="M-15 -80 H15 V-42 H36 L0 0 L-36 -42 H-15 Z" fill="#e0457b" stroke="#e0457b" stroke-width="12" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
  <path d="M-6 -74 V-48" stroke="#f58db0" stroke-width="7" stroke-linecap="round"/>`;   // pointing down, tip at 0 0
function drawRoom() {
  let s = `<image href="${ART.room}" x="0" y="0" width="1984" height="1116"/><g id="extras">${extrasSvg()}</g>`;
  for (const [id, t] of Object.entries(TARGETS)) {
    s += `<g class="tgt" data-t="${id}" tabindex="0" role="button" aria-label="${t.name}">
      <rect x="${t.x}" y="${t.y}" width="${t.w}" height="${t.h}" rx="40" fill="rgba(255,255,255,0)"/>
      ${SHAPES[id] ? `<image class="ring" href="${SHAPES[id]}" x="${t.x - 16}" y="${t.y - 16}" width="${t.w + 32}" height="${t.h + 32}"/>` : ringSvg(t.x + 6, t.y + 6, t.w - 12, t.h - 12)}${tagSvg(t)}</g>`;
  }
  s += `<g id="petLayer"></g>
    <g id="sinkFront" style="opacity:0; pointer-events:none"><clipPath id="sinkClip"><path d="${SINK_FRONT}"/></clipPath><image href="${ART.room}" x="0" y="0" width="1984" height="1116" clip-path="url(#sinkClip)"/></g>
    <g id="tubFront" style="opacity:0; pointer-events:none"><clipPath id="tubClip"><path d="${TUB_FRONT}"/></clipPath><image href="${ART.room}" x="0" y="0" width="1984" height="1116" clip-path="url(#tubClip)"/></g>
    <g id="treeFront" style="opacity:0; pointer-events:none"><clipPath id="treeClip"><path d="${TREE_FRONT}"/></clipPath><image href="${ART.room}" x="0" y="0" width="1984" height="1116" clip-path="url(#treeClip)"/></g>
    <g id="pickArrow" class="pick-arrow"><g transform="translate(492 146)">${ARROW}</g></g>
    <g class="readout" id="readout"><rect x="${ON_SCALE.cx - 95}" y="300" width="190" height="72" rx="36" fill="#fff" stroke="#4a9be0" stroke-width="6"/>
      <text id="readNum" x="${ON_SCALE.cx}" y="350" text-anchor="middle" font-size="40" font-weight="bold" fill="#4a3b5c"></text></g>`;
  room.innerHTML = s;
}

function setTask(i, quiet) {
  task = i;
  const t = TASKS[task];   // after the last task there is none: everything is done
  drawTasks(!quiet);
  const doneT = TASKS.slice(0, Math.min(task, TASKS.length)).flatMap(x => x.targets).concat(fed ? ['food'] : [], watered ? ['water'] : []);
  room.querySelectorAll('.tgt').forEach(g => g.classList.toggle('done', doneT.includes(g.dataset.t)));
  const pickTime = task === 0;   // picking a pet: an arrow points at the crates instead of a glow
  room.querySelectorAll('.tgt').forEach(g => { const mine = !!t && t.targets.includes(g.dataset.t); g.classList.toggle('on', mine && !pickTime); g.classList.toggle('named', mine && pickTime); });
  document.getElementById('pickArrow').style.display = pickTime ? '' : 'none';
  room.querySelectorAll('[data-t="dogs"], [data-t="cats"]').forEach(g => g.classList.toggle('swap', !!pet));
  const pg = document.querySelector('.petg');
  if (pg) pg.classList.toggle('drag', !!t && ['scale', 'bubbles', 'brush'].includes(t.icon));   // drag the pet to the scale or the tub
  if (t) guide(say(t.guide)); else guideEl.classList.add('hide');   // all done: the All Done card says it
}

const guideEl = document.getElementById('guide');
function guide(text) {
  if (!guideEl.textContent) { guideEl.textContent = text; return; }
  guideEl.classList.add('hide');
  setTimeout(() => { guideEl.textContent = text; guideEl.classList.remove('hide'); }, 250);
}

function nudgeGuide() {   // a little bounce on the line that already says what to do
  guideEl.classList.remove('nudge'); void guideEl.offsetWidth; guideEl.classList.add('nudge');
  const a = document.getElementById('pickArrow'); if (a && a.style.display !== 'none') { a.classList.remove('nudge'); void a.getBBox(); a.classList.add('nudge'); }
}
let toastTimer;
function showToast(msg, ms) {
  const t = document.getElementById('toast');
  t.textContent = msg; t.classList.add('show');
  clearTimeout(toastTimer); toastTimer = setTimeout(() => t.classList.remove('show'), Math.max(ms || 0, tbReadMs(msg)));
}

function useTarget(g) {
  if (busy) return;
  const id = g.dataset.t;
  if ((id === 'dogs' || id === 'cats') && pet) {   // a pet is out: tap a crate to pick a different one (only while it sits on the rug)
    if (drag || wdrag || wd || document.querySelector('.overlay.open')) return;
    return openPicker(id === 'dogs' ? 'dog' : 'cat');
  }
  if (g.classList.contains('done')) return;   // already done
  if (!TASKS[task]) return;
  const allowed = TASKS[task].targets.includes(id) || (id === 'water' && fed && !watered);
  if (!allowed) return id === 'water' && TASKS[task].icon === 'bowl' && !fed ? showToast(`Feed ${pet.name} first!`) : nudgeGuide();   // not this one yet
  if (id === 'dogs' || id === 'cats') return openPicker(id === 'dogs' ? 'dog' : 'cat');
  if (id === 'scale') return weigh();
  if (id === 'food') return fed ? null : kind === 'cat' ? openCatFood() : openMeasure();
  if (id === 'water') return fillWater();
  if (id === 'bath') return goToBath();
  if (id === 'tree') return playAtTree();
  g.classList.remove('shake'); void g.getBBox(); g.classList.add('shake');   // not built yet
  setTimeout(() => g.classList.remove('shake'), 500);
}

// ---------- Picker ----------
const picker = document.getElementById('picker'), strip = document.getElementById('pkStrip'), nextBtn = document.getElementById('pkNext');
const EMPTY_PAW = `<svg viewBox="-24 -26 48 48">${ICONS.paw.replace(/#8a5a3b/g, '#c9b8dc')}</svg>`;
function openOverlay(el) { el.classList.add('open'); el.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'false'); }
function closeOverlay(el) { el.classList.remove('open'); el.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true'); }

function openPicker(k) {
  kind = k; page = 0;
  if (!pet) { TASKS = TASK_LISTS[k]; nameTarget('bath', k === 'cat' ? 'Groom' : 'Bath'); setTask(0, true); }   // picking a cat shows the cat list
  const list = PETS[k], pages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(list.length / 8));
  document.getElementById('pkTitle').textContent = k === 'dog' ? 'Pick a dog' : 'Pick a cat';
  let s = '';
  for (let p = 0; p < pages; p++) {
    s += '<div class="pk-page">';
    for (let i = p * 8; i < p * 8 + 8; i++) {
      const a = list[i];
      s += a ? `<button class="pk-card" data-pet="${a.id}" aria-label="${a.name}"><img src="${ART[a.art]}" alt="" style="height: ${kind === 'cat' ? 75 : Math.round(Math.min(90, 100 * sizeK(a) ** 2.2))}%"><span class="pk-name">${a.name}</span><span class="pk-sex" aria-hidden="true">${PAW(a.boy ? '#4a9be0' : '#e0457b')}</span></button>`
             : `<div class="pk-card empty" aria-hidden="true">${EMPTY_PAW}</div>`;
    }
    s += '</div>';
  }
  strip.innerHTML = s;
  strip.style.transition = 'none'; strip.style.transform = 'translateX(0)'; void strip.offsetWidth; strip.style.transition = '';
  nextBtn.disabled = pages < 2;
  openOverlay(picker);
}

nextBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
  const pages = strip.children.length;
  if (pages < 2) return;
  page = (page + 1) % pages;
  strip.style.transform = `translateX(${-100 * page}%)`;
});
function closePicker() { closeOverlay(picker); if (pet) kind = pet.kind; }   // not picking after all: the pet you have stays
document.getElementById('pkClose').addEventListener('click', closePicker);
picker.addEventListener('click', e => {
  if (e.target === picker) return closePicker();
  const c = e.target.closest('.pk-card[data-pet]');
  if (c) openInfo(PETS[kind].find(a => a.id === c.dataset.pet), c);
});

// ---------- A pet's card: grows out of its picture in the picker ----------
const PAW = col => `<svg viewBox="0 0 100 92"><ellipse cx="39" cy="18" rx="10" ry="14" fill="${col}"/><ellipse cx="62" cy="18" rx="10" ry="14" fill="${col}"/><ellipse cx="18" cy="42" rx="9" ry="13" fill="${col}" transform="rotate(-20 18 42)"/><ellipse cx="82" cy="42" rx="9" ry="13" fill="${col}" transform="rotate(20 82 42)"/><path d="M50 44 C32 44 22 66 24 78 C26 88 38 86 50 84 C62 86 74 88 76 78 C78 66 68 44 50 44 Z" fill="${col}"/></svg>`;
let infoPet = null;
function openInfo(a, card) {
  infoPet = a;
  const el = document.getElementById('pkInfo'), r = card.getBoundingClientRect(), pr = el.parentElement.getBoundingClientRect();
  el.style.transformOrigin = `${r.left + r.width / 2 - pr.left}px ${r.top + r.height / 2 - pr.top}px`;
  document.getElementById('piPic').src = ART[a.art];
  document.getElementById('piName').textContent = a.name;
  document.getElementById('piFacts').innerHTML = `
    <div class="pi-row"><span class="pi-paw">${PAW(a.boy ? '#4a9be0' : '#e0457b')}</span>${a.boy ? 'Boy' : 'Girl'}</div>
    <div class="pi-row"><b>Breed:</b>&nbsp;${a.breed}</div>
    <div class="pi-row"><b>Loves:</b>&nbsp;${a.loves}</div>`;
  document.getElementById('piPick').textContent = `Pick ${a.name}`;
  el.hidden = false; el.classList.remove('open'); void el.offsetWidth; el.classList.add('open');
}
function closeInfo() { const el = document.getElementById('pkInfo'); el.classList.remove('open'); setTimeout(() => { el.hidden = true; }, 250); }
document.getElementById('piBack').addEventListener('click', closeInfo);
document.getElementById('piPick').addEventListener('click', () => { const a = infoPet; closeInfo(); choosePet(a); });

function clearPet() {   // the pet goes back to its crate and the room is put back (nothing is paid)
  pet = null; fed = false; watered = false;
  room.querySelectorAll('.tgt.used').forEach(g => g.classList.remove('used'));
  document.getElementById('treeFront').style.opacity = 0;
  document.getElementById('readout').classList.remove('show');
  document.getElementById('petLayer').innerHTML = '';
  document.getElementById('extras').innerHTML = extrasSvg();   // everything back in its place
}
function choosePet(a) {
  if (pet) clearPet();   // picking a different pet partway through: start over with the new one
  pet = Object.assign({ kind }, a); closeOverlay(picker);
  hideExtra('crate-' + a.id);   // out of its crate (if it was napping in one)
  const pic = new Image();
  pic.onload = () => {
    const h = rugH(), w = h * pic.width / pic.height, x = RUG.cx - w / 2, y = RUG.bottom - h;
    document.getElementById('petLayer').innerHTML =
      `<g class="petg" role="button" tabindex="0" aria-label="${a.name}">${ringSvg(x - 24, y - 20, w + 48, h + 36)}
        <rect class="hit" x="${x - 24}" y="${y - 20}" width="${w + 48}" height="${h + 36}" rx="40" fill="rgba(255,255,255,0)"/>
        <image class="pet in" href="${ART[a.art]}" x="${x}" y="${y}" width="${w}" height="${h}"/></g>`;
    TASKS = TASK_LISTS[kind]; nameTarget('bath', kind === 'cat' ? 'Groom' : 'Bath');
    setTask(1);
  };
  pic.src = ART[a.art];
}

// ---------- Weigh: drag the pet onto the scale (or tap the scale) ----------
function petMove(dx, dy, k, ms) {
  const g = document.querySelector('.petg');
  g.style.transition = ms ? `transform ${ms}ms ease-in-out` : 'none';
  g.style.transformOrigin = `${RUG.cx}px ${RUG.bottom}px`;
  g.style.transform = `translate(${dx}px, ${dy}px) scale(${k})`;
}
function svgPoint(e) {
  const p = room.createSVGPoint(); p.x = e.clientX; p.y = e.clientY;
  return p.matrixTransform(room.getScreenCTM().inverse());
}
let drag = null;
room.addEventListener('pointerdown', e => {
  const g = e.target.closest('.petg.drag');
  if (!g || busy) return;
  drag = { start: svgPoint(e), moved: false, dx: 0, dy: 0 }; g.classList.add('dragging');
  room.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
});
room.addEventListener('pointermove', e => {
  if (!drag) return;
  const p = svgPoint(e), dx = p.x - drag.start.x, dy = p.y - drag.start.y;
  if (Math.hypot(dx, dy) > 8) drag.moved = true;
  petMove(dx, dy, dragK(dx, dy), 0);
  drag.dx = dx; drag.dy = dy; drag.p = p;
  dropHint(petDropSpot(dx, dy, p));   // the spot glows brighter while the pet is right on it; let go to drop
});
function dragK(dx, dy) {   // 1 on the rug, the spot's size once the feet reach the spot
  const t = TASKS[task], to = t && t.icon === 'scale' ? ON_SCALE : t && (t.icon === 'bubbles' || t.icon === 'brush') ? IN_TUB : null;
  if (!to) return 1;
  const all = Math.hypot(to.cx - RUG.cx, to.bottom - RUG.bottom), left = Math.hypot(to.cx - RUG.cx - dx, to.bottom - RUG.bottom - dy);
  return to.k + (1 - to.k) * Math.min(1, left / all);
}
function dropHint(id) { room.querySelectorAll('.tgt').forEach(g => g.classList.toggle('over', g.dataset.t === id)); }
// Drag and drop rule for the whole game (Mac, Nov 23): nothing pulls or jumps while you drag.
// The spot glows brighter when the thing is right on it, and it only counts when you let go there;
// then it glides gently into place.
const on = (t, x, y, below = 0) => x > t.x && x < t.x + t.w && y > t.y && y < t.y + t.h + below;
function petDropSpot(dx, dy, p) {
  const fx = RUG.cx + dx, fy = RUG.bottom + dy, t = TASKS[task];   // where the pet's feet are
  if (t && t.icon === 'scale' && on(TARGETS.scale, fx, fy, 25)) return 'scale';
  if (t && (t.icon === 'bubbles' || t.icon === 'brush') && (on(TARGETS.bath, fx, fy) || (p && on(TARGETS.bath, p.x, p.y)))) return 'bath';   // anywhere in the glowing tub box
  return null;
}
room.addEventListener('pointerup', () => {
  if (!drag) return;
  const d = drag; drag = null;
  document.querySelector('.petg').classList.remove('dragging'); dropHint(null);
  if (!d.moved) return;
  const spot = petDropSpot(d.dx, d.dy, d.p);
  if (spot === 'scale') weigh();
  else if (spot === 'bath') goToBath();
  else petMove(0, 0, 1, 450);   // not on the right spot: back to the rug, full size
});

function weigh() {
  if (busy || TASKS[task].icon !== 'scale') return;
  busy = true;
  document.querySelector('.petg').classList.remove('drag');
  room.querySelector('[data-t="scale"]').classList.remove('on');
  guideEl.classList.add('hide');
  petMove(ON_SCALE.cx - RUG.cx, ON_SCALE.bottom - RUG.bottom, ON_SCALE.k, 600);
  const ro = document.getElementById('readout'), num = document.getElementById('readNum');
  num.textContent = '0 lb';
  setTimeout(() => {
    ro.classList.add('show');
    let n = 0; const steps = 12;
    const tick = setInterval(() => {
      n++; num.textContent = `${Math.round(pet.weight * n / steps)} lb`;
      if (n >= steps) { clearInterval(tick); setTimeout(showWeightCard, 950); }
    }, 55);
  }, 650);
}

function cupSvg(cups) {   // the same measuring cup as the pouring screen, filled with food to this much
  const lines = [.25, .5, .75, 1].map(c => { const y = cupY(c), t = (CUP.y0 - y) / (CUP.y0 - CUP.y1), l = CUP.left0 + (CUP.left1 - CUP.left0) * t;
    return `<rect x="${l + 4}" y="${y - 4}" width="66" height="8" rx="4" fill="#4f86b8"/>`; }).join('');
  return `<svg viewBox="${CUP.x - 6} ${CUP.y - 6} ${CUP.w + 12} ${CUP.h + 12}"><image href="${ART.cup}" x="${CUP.x}" y="${CUP.y}" width="${CUP.w}" height="${CUP.h}"/>
    <g opacity=".88"><path d="${cupInside(cups)}" fill="#c98a5a"/>${kibbleDots(cups)}</g>${lines}</svg>`;
}

function showWeightCard() {
  const rule = foodFor(pet, kind);
  pet.food = rule.cups;   // the food step will use this
  document.getElementById('wtTitle').textContent = `${pet.name} weighs`;
  document.getElementById('wtBig').textContent = `${pet.weight} pounds`;
  document.getElementById('wtSays').innerHTML = `So ${pet.name} eats <b>${CUPS[rule.cups]}</b> of food.`;
  document.getElementById('wtChart').innerHTML = FOOD[kind].map(r =>
    `<div class="wt-col${r === rule ? ' me' : ''}">${cupSvg(r.cups)}<b>${CUPS[r.cups]}</b><span>${r.label}</span></div>`).join('');
  openOverlay(document.getElementById('weightCard'));
}
document.getElementById('wtOk').addEventListener('click', () => {
  closeOverlay(document.getElementById('weightCard'));
  document.getElementById('readout').classList.remove('show');
  petMove(0, 0, 1, 600);   // back to the rug
  setTimeout(() => { busy = false; setTask(2); }, 650);
});


// ---------- Food and water ----------
// Measuring: a short look at the measuring cup, then hold the food bin to pour and let go at the pet's line.
// The bowl only shows up when the food is just right; then drag the cup to the bowl and hold it there to pour.
// After eating, drag the empty water bowl to the sink to fill it.
const POUR_RATE = .12;     // cups per second while holding the bin (1 cup takes about 8 seconds)
const CLOSE_ENOUGH = .045; // how near the line counts as just right
// Layout (1100 wide): the bowl on the left (the cup's lip faces it), the cup in the middle, the food bin on the right.
const CUP = { x: 340, y: 150, w: 360, h: 307, y0: 404, y1: 228, left0: 410, left1: 382, right0: 578, right1: 596 };
const CUP_C = [520, 300];                 // the cup turns around its middle when it pours
const CUP_S = 1.22, CUP_CS = [520, 205 + (300 - 205) * CUP_S];   // the cup is drawn 1.22 times bigger, grown from its opening (520, 205)
const MS_BOWL = { x: 40, y: 360, w: 180 };
const BOWL_OPEN = { cx: .53, cy: .30, rx: .35, ry: .18 };   // the bowl's opening, as parts of the bowl picture
const EAT = { h: 330, cx: 1010, nose: [.15, .86] };       // the eating picture on the rug, and where its nose is
const FLOOR_BOWL = 157.5;                                 // bowls on the floor (5% bigger than before)
let ms = null, fed = false, watered = false;

const cupY = c => CUP.y0 - (CUP.y0 - CUP.y1) * c;
function cupInside(c) {   // the glass's inside from the bottom up to c cups (it is a little wider at the top)
  const y = cupY(c), t = (CUP.y0 - y) / (CUP.y0 - CUP.y1);
  const l = CUP.left0 + (CUP.left1 - CUP.left0) * t, r = CUP.right0 + (CUP.right1 - CUP.right0) * t;
  return `M${CUP.left0},${CUP.y0 - 10} Q${CUP.left0 + 8},${CUP.y0 + 4} ${(CUP.left0 + CUP.right0) / 2},${CUP.y0 + 5} Q${CUP.right0 - 8},${CUP.y0 + 4} ${CUP.right0},${CUP.y0 - 10} L${r},${y} Q${(l + r) / 2},${y - 6} ${l},${y} Z`;
}
function kibbleDots(c) {
  let s = '';
  if (c <= 0) return s;
  const top = cupY(c);
  for (let i = 0; i < 26; i++) {
    const y = CUP.y0 - 10 - ((i * 37) % 100) / 100 * (CUP.y0 - 10 - top), x = CUP.left0 + 16 + ((i * 53) % (CUP.right0 - CUP.left0 - 32));
    if (y > top + 6) s += `<circle cx="${x}" cy="${y}" r="5" fill="#a86d43"/>`;
  }
  return s;
}

function binSvg() {
  return `<g class="ms-bin" id="msBin" role="button" tabindex="0" aria-label="Food bin, hold to pour" transform="translate(760 0)">
    ${ringSvg(40, 70, 290, 420)}
    <g class="tilt">
      <rect x="70" y="150" width="230" height="320" rx="34" fill="#e3eef0"/>
      <path d="M78 250 Q120 232 160 250 T240 248 T292 250 V440 Q292 462 270 462 H100 Q78 462 78 440 Z" fill="#b98557"/>
      <circle cx="120" cy="300" r="7" fill="#9a6a42"/><circle cx="190" cy="340" r="7" fill="#9a6a42"/><circle cx="245" cy="290" r="7" fill="#9a6a42"/><circle cx="150" cy="400" r="7" fill="#9a6a42"/><circle cx="230" cy="410" r="7" fill="#9a6a42"/>
      <rect x="92" y="170" width="22" height="270" rx="11" fill="#fff" opacity=".45"/>
      <g class="lid"><rect x="58" y="118" width="254" height="44" rx="20" fill="#c4a690"/><rect x="160" y="96" width="50" height="30" rx="14" fill="#b39581"/></g>
    </g>
    ${[0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7].map(i => `<circle class="kib" cx="${-238 + (i % 4) * 9}" cy="194" r="${7 - (i % 3)}" fill="#a86d43" style="--dx:${(i % 3) * 4 - 4}px;--dy:${150 + (i % 4) * 12}px;--d:${i * .07}s"/>`).join('')}
  </g>`;
}

function drawMeasure() {
  const lines = [.25, .5, .75, 1], bh = MS_BOWL.w * 236 / 360;
  let s = '';
  s += `<g id="msBowl" class="away"><image href="${ART.bowlFood}" x="${MS_BOWL.x}" y="${MS_BOWL.y}" width="${MS_BOWL.w}" height="${bh}"/>
    <ellipse id="msBowlFood" cx="${MS_BOWL.x + MS_BOWL.w * BOWL_OPEN.cx}" cy="${MS_BOWL.y + bh * BOWL_OPEN.cy}" rx="${MS_BOWL.w * BOWL_OPEN.rx * .9}" ry="${bh * BOWL_OPEN.ry * .9}" fill="#b98557" opacity="0"/></g>`;
  s += `<g id="msCupMove"><g transform="translate(520 205) scale(1.22) translate(-520 -205)"><g class="ms-cup" id="msCup" role="button" tabindex="0" aria-label="Measuring cup">
    ${ringSvg(CUP.x - 4, CUP.y - 10, CUP.w + 8, CUP.h + 20)}
    <image href="${ART.cup}" x="${CUP.x}" y="${CUP.y}" width="${CUP.w}" height="${CUP.h}"/>
    <g opacity=".88"><path id="msFood" d="" fill="#c98a5a"/><g id="msDots"></g></g>
`;
  lines.forEach(c => {
    const y = cupY(c), t = (CUP.y0 - y) / (CUP.y0 - CUP.y1), l = CUP.left0 + (CUP.left1 - CUP.left0) * t;
    s += `<g class="ms-line" data-c="${c}">
      <rect class="glow" x="${l - 6}" y="${y - 9}" width="86" height="18" rx="9" fill="#fff6b0"/>
      <rect x="${l + 4}" y="${y - 3}" width="66" height="6" rx="3" fill="#4f86b8"/>
      <g class="lbl"><rect x="${l - 112}" y="${y - 17}" width="96" height="34" rx="17" fill="#fff" stroke="#4f86b8" stroke-width="4"/>
      <text x="${l - 64}" y="${y + 8}" text-anchor="middle" font-size="22" font-weight="bold" fill="#2f6390">${CUPS[c]}</text></g></g>`;
  });
  s += `</g></g></g><g id="msStream"></g><g id="msBinWrap" class="away">${binSvg()}</g>`;
  document.getElementById('msStage').innerHTML = s;
  setCup(0);
}
function setCup(c) {
  ms.amount = Math.max(0, Math.min(1.12, c));
  document.getElementById('msFood').setAttribute('d', ms.amount > 0 ? cupInside(ms.amount) : '');
  document.getElementById('msDots').innerHTML = kibbleDots(ms.amount);
  const tipped = document.getElementById('msCup').classList.contains('tip');
}
const msSay = t => { document.getElementById('msSay').innerHTML = t; };
function msButtons(next, spoon) {
  const n = document.getElementById('msNext'), sp = document.getElementById('msSpoon');
  n.hidden = !next; if (next) n.textContent = next;
  sp.hidden = !spoon; sp.classList.toggle('on', spoon === 'on');
}
const msShow = (id, on) => document.getElementById(id).classList.toggle('away', !on);

function openMeasure() {
  hideExtra('shelfFood');   // the bowl comes off the shelf
  ms = { step: 0, amount: 0, pouring: false, done: false, ready: false, drag: null };
  document.getElementById('msSay').style.display = '';   // (cat food hides the words)
  document.getElementById('msTitle').textContent = `Measure ${pet.name}'s food`;
  drawMeasure();
  openOverlay(document.getElementById('measure'));
  lessonStep();
}
const MS_VIEW = { full: [0, 0, 1100, 520], cup: [150, 110, 760, 415] };
function msView(to, ms_) {   // move the measuring screen's view smoothly (bigger cup at first)
  const st = document.getElementById('msStage'), from = (st.getAttribute('viewBox') || '').split(' ').map(Number);
  if (!ms_ || from.length !== 4) return st.setAttribute('viewBox', to.join(' '));
  const t0 = performance.now();
  const step = t => { const k = Math.min(1, (t - t0) / ms_), e = k < .5 ? 2 * k * k : 1 - Math.pow(-2 * k + 2, 2) / 2;
    st.setAttribute('viewBox', from.map((v, i) => v + (to[i] - v) * e).join(' ')); if (k < 1) requestAnimationFrame(step); };
  requestAnimationFrame(step);
}
function lessonStep() {
  const goal = CUPS[pet.food];
  if (ms.step === 0) {   // just the cup first, big
    msView(MS_VIEW.cup);
    document.querySelectorAll('.ms-line').forEach((g, i) => setTimeout(() => g.classList.add('show'), 300 + i * 250));
    msSay('This is a measuring cup. The lines show how much food is inside.');
    msButtons('Next');
  } else {               // then the food bin, and the line to fill to
    msView(MS_VIEW.full, 500);
    document.querySelector(`.ms-line[data-c="${pet.food}"]`).classList.add('goal');
    msShow('msBinWrap', true);
    document.getElementById('msBin').classList.add('on');
    msSay(`${pet.name} needs <b>${goal}</b>. Hold the food bin to pour, and let go at the ${goal} line.`);
    msButtons(null);
  }
}
document.getElementById('msNext').addEventListener('click', () => { ms.step++; lessonStep(); });
document.getElementById('msClose').addEventListener('click', () => { stopPour(); closeOverlay(document.getElementById('measure')); });

function startPour(e) {
  if (!ms || ms.step < 1 || ms.done || ms.pouring) return;
  if (e) e.preventDefault();
  ms.pouring = true; ms.last = performance.now() + 550;   // the food starts once the bin is over the cup
  ready(false);
  document.getElementById('msBin').classList.add('pour');
  document.getElementById('msStage').classList.add('pouring');
  requestAnimationFrame(pourTick);
}
function pourTick(t) {
  if (!ms || !ms.pouring) return;
  if (t > ms.last) { setCup(ms.amount + POUR_RATE * (t - ms.last) / 1000); ms.last = t; }
  requestAnimationFrame(pourTick);
}
function stopPour() {
  if (!ms || !ms.pouring) return;
  ms.pouring = false;
  document.getElementById('msBin').classList.remove('pour');
  document.getElementById('msStage').classList.remove('pouring');
  judge();
}
// is the food at the line? Only then does the bowl show up.
function judge() {
  const goal = CUPS[pet.food], diff = ms.amount - pet.food;
  if (ms.amount <= 0) { ready(false); msButtons(null); msSay(`Hold the food bin to pour, and let go at the ${goal} line.`); return; }
  if (Math.abs(diff) <= CLOSE_ENOUGH) {
    ready(true); msButtons(null);
    msSay(`Just right! Now drag the cup to the bowl and hold it there to pour.`);
  } else if (diff < 0) {
    ready(false); msButtons(null);   // no spoon when there is too little
    msSay(`A little more. Pour up to the ${goal} line.`);
  } else {
    ready(false); msButtons(null, 'on');
    msSay(`Too much. Take some out with the spoon.`);
  }
}
function ready(on) {
  ms.ready = on;
  msShow('msBowl', on);
  document.getElementById('msCup').classList.toggle('on', on);
  document.getElementById('msBin').classList.toggle('on', !on && !ms.done);
}
const stage = document.getElementById('msStage');
stage.addEventListener('pointerdown', e => {
  if (e.target.closest('#msBin')) { stage.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); return startPour(e); }
  if (e.target.closest('#msCup') && ms && ms.ready && !ms.done) {   // pick up the cup
    e.preventDefault();
    const p = msPoint(e);
    ms.drag = { sx: p.x, sy: p.y, dx: 0, dy: 0 }; document.getElementById('msCup').classList.add('held');
    document.getElementById('msCupMove').style.transition = 'none';
    stage.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
    ms.last = performance.now(); requestAnimationFrame(pourOutTick);
  }
});
stage.addEventListener('pointermove', e => {
  if (!ms || !ms.drag) return;
  const p = msPoint(e), d = ms.drag;
  d.dx = p.x - d.sx; d.dy = p.y - d.sy;
  // anywhere near the bowl counts; then the cup snaps so its lip is right over the bowl and the food lands in it
  const cx = CUP_CS[0] + d.dx, cy = CUP_CS[1] + d.dy, bcx = MS_BOWL.x + MS_BOWL.w / 2;
  d.over = cx > bcx + 10 && cx < bcx + 340 && cy > MS_BOWL.y - 360 && cy < MS_BOWL.y + 160;
  const mv = document.getElementById('msCupMove');
  let tx = d.dx, ty = d.dy;
  if (d.over) { tx = bcx + 10 + 158 * CUP_S - CUP_CS[0]; ty = MS_BOWL.y - 28 - 113 * CUP_S - CUP_CS[1]; }
  mv.style.transition = d.over ? 'transform .18s ease-out' : 'none';
  mv.style.transform = `translate(${tx}px, ${ty}px)`;
  document.getElementById('msCup').classList.toggle('tip', d.over);
  d.lip = [CUP_CS[0] + tx - 158 * CUP_S, CUP_CS[1] + ty + 113 * CUP_S];   // where the lip is once the cup tips
});
function dropCup() {
  if (!ms) return;
  stopPour();
  if (!ms.drag) return;
  ms.drag = null;
  if (ms.done) return;
  const mv = document.getElementById('msCupMove');
  document.getElementById('msCup').classList.remove('tip');
  document.getElementById('msStream').innerHTML = '';
  mv.style.transition = 'transform .35s'; mv.style.transform = 'translate(0px, 0px)';
  if (ms.amount < pet.food - CLOSE_ENOUGH && ms.amount > 0) msSay('Keep the cup over the bowl until all the food is in.');
}
stage.addEventListener('pointerup', dropCup);
stage.addEventListener('pointercancel', dropCup);
function msPoint(e) {
  const p = stage.createSVGPoint(); p.x = e.clientX; p.y = e.clientY;
  return p.matrixTransform(stage.getScreenCTM().inverse());
}
const BOWL_FILL = .45;   // cups per second out of the cup while it is tipped over the bowl
function pourOutTick(t) {
  if (!ms || !ms.drag || ms.done) return;
  const d = ms.drag, dt = (t - ms.last) / 1000; ms.last = t;
  const stream = document.getElementById('msStream');
  if (d.over && ms.amount > 0) {
    setCup(ms.amount - BOWL_FILL * dt);
    document.getElementById('msBowlFood').setAttribute('opacity', Math.min(1, 1 - ms.amount / pet.food));
    if (!stream.innerHTML) stream.innerHTML = [0, 1, 2, 3, 4].map(i =>
      `<circle class="kib2" cx="${d.lip[0] + (i % 2) * 8}" cy="${d.lip[1]}" r="${6 - (i % 3)}" fill="#a86d43" style="animation-delay:${i * .07}s"/>`).join('');
    else stream.querySelectorAll('circle').forEach((c, i) => { c.setAttribute('cx', d.lip[0] + (i % 2) * 8); c.setAttribute('cy', d.lip[1]); });
    if (ms.amount <= 0) return foodInBowl();
  } else stream.innerHTML = '';
  requestAnimationFrame(pourOutTick);
}
function foodInBowl() {
  ms.done = true; ms.drag = null;
  document.getElementById('msStream').innerHTML = '';
  document.querySelectorAll('#msCup .spill').forEach(e => { e.style.opacity = 0; });
  document.getElementById('msBowlFood').setAttribute('opacity', 1);
  document.getElementById('msCup').classList.remove('on');
  msSay(`Just right! That's ${CUPS[pet.food]} of food for ${pet.name}.`);
  setTimeout(() => {
    closeOverlay(document.getElementById('measure'));
    feedPet();
  }, 1200);
}
document.getElementById('msSpoon').addEventListener('click', () => { setCup(ms.amount - .05); judge(); });
stage.addEventListener('keydown', e => { if (e.target.closest('#msBin') && (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ')) startPour(e); });
stage.addEventListener('keyup', e => { if (e.target.closest('#msBin')) stopPour(); });

// in the room: the bowl goes in front of the pet and it eats
function feedPet() {
  fed = true;
  room.querySelector('[data-t="food"]').classList.remove('on');
  const EH = eatH(), left = EAT.cx - EH * 647 / 440 / 2, top = RUG.bottom - EH;
  const nose = pet.nose || EAT.nose, nx = left + EH * 647 / 440 * nose[0], ny = top + EH * nose[1];
  // the bowl goes behind the pet, and a copy of its front rim goes in front, so the face dips into the bowl
  const bw = FLOOR_BOWL, bh = bw * 236 / 360, bx = nx - bw / 2 - 10, by = Math.min(ny - bh * BOWL_OPEN.cy - 16, RUG.bottom + 12 - bh);   // never above the floor
  const ocy = by + bh * BOWL_OPEN.cy, ory = bh * BOWL_OPEN.ry;
  const layer = document.getElementById('petLayer');
  layer.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin', `<g id="foodBowl" class="bowlfood"><image href="${ART.bowlFood}" x="${bx}" y="${by}" width="${bw}" height="${bh}"/>
    <ellipse id="bowlKib" cx="${bx + bw * BOWL_OPEN.cx}" cy="${ocy}" rx="${bw * BOWL_OPEN.rx * .85}" ry="${ory * .8}" fill="${kind === 'cat' ? '#a36b48' : '#b98557'}"/></g>`);
  layer.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', `<g id="foodRim" class="bowlfood" clip-path="url(#rimClip)"><clipPath id="rimClip"><rect x="${bx - 5}" y="${ocy + ory * .55}" width="${bw + 10}" height="${bh}"/></clipPath>
    <image href="${ART.bowlFood}" x="${bx}" y="${by}" width="${bw}" height="${bh}"/></g>`);
  setPose('eat');
  guide(`${pet.name} is eating.`);
  busy = true;
  setTimeout(() => { document.getElementById('bowlKib').setAttribute('opacity', 0); }, 2600);
  setTimeout(() => {
    setPose('sit');
    ['foodBowl', 'foodRim'].forEach(id => { const fb = document.getElementById(id); fb.style.opacity = 0; setTimeout(() => fb.remove(), 450); });
    busy = false; waterTime();
  }, 3200);
}
function setPose(p) {
  const img = document.querySelector('.petg image'), ring = document.querySelector('.petg .ring');
  if (p === 'eat') {
    const EH = eatH(), w = EH * 647 / 440;
    img.setAttribute('href', ART[pet.eat]); img.setAttribute('x', EAT.cx - w / 2); img.setAttribute('y', RUG.bottom - EH);
    img.setAttribute('width', w); img.setAttribute('height', EH);
  } else {
    const h = rugH(), w = h * 443 / 560;
    img.setAttribute('href', ART[pet.art]); img.setAttribute('x', RUG.cx - w / 2); img.setAttribute('y', RUG.bottom - h);
    img.setAttribute('width', w); img.setAttribute('height', h);
  }
  ring.style.display = 'none';
}

// water: after eating, an empty bowl sits by the pet; drag it to the sink (or tap the sink) and it fills
const WB = { x: 1180, bottom: RUG.bottom + 12 }, SINK_SPOT = { cx: 1512, bottom: 506, k: .7 };   // in the basin, under the faucet
const SINK_FRONT = 'M1386 503 L1608 503 L1608 560 L1386 560 Z';   // the sink's front edge, drawn over the bowl
function waterTime() {
  wfill = 0;
  hideExtra('shelfWater');
  const bw = FLOOR_BOWL, bh = bw * 236 / 360, x = WB.x, y = WB.bottom - bh;
  const cx = x + bw * BOWL_OPEN.cx, cy = y + bh * BOWL_OPEN.cy, rx = bw * BOWL_OPEN.rx * .85, ry = bh * BOWL_OPEN.ry * .8;
  document.getElementById('petLayer').insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin', `<g id="waterBowl" class="wb drag" role="button" tabindex="0" aria-label="Water bowl, drag it to the sink">
    ${ringSvg(x - 18, y - 18, bw + 36, bh + 36)}
    <image href="${ART.bowlWater}" x="${x}" y="${y}" width="${bw}" height="${bh}"/>
    <ellipse id="wbWater" cx="${cx}" cy="${cy}" rx="${rx}" ry="${ry}" fill="#8fd0f5" style="transform-box: fill-box; transform-origin: center; transform: scale(0); transition: transform .9s ease-out"/>
    <ellipse id="wbShine" cx="${cx - rx * .35}" cy="${cy - ry * .25}" rx="${rx * .25}" ry="${ry * .2}" fill="#fff" opacity="0" style="transition: opacity .4s .6s"/></g>`);
  room.querySelector('[data-t="water"]').classList.add('on');
  guide(`Drag the water bowl to the sink.`);
}
function wbMove(dx, dy, ms_, k = 1) {
  const g = document.getElementById('waterBowl');
  g.style.transition = ms_ ? `transform ${ms_}ms ease-in-out` : 'none';
  g.style.transformOrigin = `${WB.x + FLOOR_BOWL / 2}px ${WB.bottom}px`;
  g.style.transform = `translate(${dx}px, ${dy}px) scale(${k})`;
}
let wdrag = null;
addEventListener('pointerup', () => setTimeout(() => document.querySelectorAll('.held').forEach(h => h.classList.remove('held')), 0));
addEventListener('pointercancel', () => document.querySelectorAll('.held').forEach(h => h.classList.remove('held')));
room.addEventListener('pointerdown', e => {
  const g = e.target.closest('.wb.drag');
  if (!g || busy || watered) return;
  wdrag = { start: svgPoint(e), dx: 0, dy: 0 }; g.classList.add('held');
  room.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
  requestAnimationFrame(holdFillTick);
});
room.addEventListener('pointermove', e => {
  if (!wdrag) return;
  const p = svgPoint(e); wdrag.dx = p.x - wdrag.start.x; wdrag.dy = p.y - wdrag.start.y;
  wbMove(wdrag.dx, wdrag.dy, 0);
  wdrag.over = atSink(wdrag.dx, wdrag.dy);
  if (wdrag.over) room.querySelector('[data-t="water"]').classList.add('used');   // at the sink: no more ring there
  faucet(wdrag.over ? WB.bottom + wdrag.dy - FLOOR_BOWL * 236 / 360 * (1 - BOWL_OPEN.cy) : null);
});
// while the bowl is held over the sink, the faucet runs and the bowl fills; full = done
const WATER_RATE = .55;   // bowls per second (about 2 seconds)
let wfill = 0;
function faucet(toY) {
  let f = document.getElementById('faucetWater');
  if (toY == null) { if (f) f.remove(); return; }
  if (!f) { room.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', `<rect id="faucetWater" x="${SINK_SPOT.cx - 5}" y="424" width="10" rx="5" fill="#8fd0f5" opacity=".9" style="pointer-events:none"/>`); f = document.getElementById('faucetWater'); }
  f.setAttribute('height', Math.max(14, toY - 424));
}
function holdFillTick(t) {
  if (!wdrag) return;
  const dt = wdrag.last ? (t - wdrag.last) / 1000 : 0; wdrag.last = t;
  if (wdrag.over) {
    wfill = Math.min(1, wfill + WATER_RATE * dt);
    const w = document.getElementById('wbWater'); w.style.transition = 'none'; w.style.transform = `scale(${wfill})`;
    if (wfill >= 1) { wdrag = null; dropHint(null); return waterDone(); }
  }
  requestAnimationFrame(holdFillTick);
}
function waterDone() {   // full: the water stops and the bowl goes back by the pet
  busy = true; faucet(null);
  const g = document.getElementById('waterBowl'); g.classList.remove('drag');
  room.querySelector('[data-t="water"]').classList.remove('on');
  document.getElementById('wbShine').setAttribute('opacity', .7);
  guideEl.classList.add('hide');
  setTimeout(() => wbMove(0, 0, 600), 250);
  setTimeout(() => { watered = true; busy = false; showToast(`Fresh water for ${pet.name}`); setTask(task + 1); }, 900);
}
function atSink(dx, dy) {   // the bowl's middle has to be over the sink's basin
  const cx = WB.x + FLOOR_BOWL / 2 + dx, by = WB.bottom + dy;
  return on({ x: 1400, y: 380, w: 200, h: 200 }, cx, by);
}
room.addEventListener('pointerup', () => {
  if (!wdrag) return;
  const d = wdrag; wdrag = null; dropHint(null); faucet(null);
  if (atSink(d.dx, d.dy)) fillWater(d.dx, d.dy);   // let go over the sink: it sits under the faucet and finishes filling
  else wbMove(0, 0, 450);   // not at the sink: back by the pet
});
function fillWater(fromDx, fromDy) {
  if (watered || busy || !fed) return;
  busy = true;
  const g = document.getElementById('waterBowl'); g.classList.remove('drag');
  room.querySelector('[data-t="water"]').classList.remove('on');
  guideEl.classList.add('hide');
  const bw = FLOOR_BOWL, tx = SINK_SPOT.cx - (WB.x + bw / 2), ty = SINK_SPOT.bottom - WB.bottom;
  if (fromDx !== undefined) wbMove(fromDx, fromDy, 0);
  requestAnimationFrame(() => wbMove(tx, ty, 500, SINK_SPOT.k));   // down into the sink, under the faucet
  setTimeout(() => {   // the sink's front edge goes in front of the bowl, water runs and the bowl fills
    document.getElementById('sinkFront').style.opacity = 1;
    room.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', `<rect id="faucetWater" x="${SINK_SPOT.cx - 5}" y="424" width="10" height="34" rx="5" fill="#8fd0f5" opacity=".9"/>`);
    const w = document.getElementById('wbWater'); w.style.transition = 'transform .9s ease-out'; w.style.transform = 'scale(1)';
    document.getElementById('wbShine').setAttribute('opacity', .7);
  }, 500);
  setTimeout(() => { const f = document.getElementById('faucetWater'); if (f) f.remove(); document.getElementById('sinkFront').style.opacity = 0; wbMove(0, 0, 600); }, 1600);   // back by the pet
  setTimeout(() => {
    watered = true; busy = false;
    showToast(`Fresh water for ${pet.name}`);
    setTask(task + 1);
  }, 2300);
}

// ---------- Bath ----------
// In the tub: rub the soap on the pet (bubbles, and the stinky lines go away), move the shower over the pet to rinse,
// rub the towel to dry, then brush. You can keep going as long as you like; when a step is done the next one blinks.
const POSES = {   // the sitting pictures at the same scale (560 = the sitting pose's height), lined up by the paws
  sit:  { art: p => p.art,  w: 443, h: 560, dx: 0 },
  bath: { art: p => p.bath, w: 464, h: 580, dx: 10 },
  done: { art: p => p.done, w: 452, h: 560, dx: 4 },
  play: { art: p => p.play, w: 464, h: 580, dx: 0 },   // cats: sitting up, reaching for the feather
  playDown: { art: p => p.playDown, w: 464, h: 580, dx: 0 },   // the same play picture with the paw down (sized like play, so they line up)
};
const TUB_DOG = { cx: 560, bottom: 470, h: 380 };
const tubBottom = () => TUB_DOG.bottom + (kind === 'cat' ? 0 : 40);   // dogs sit a little deeper in the tub, so their paws are behind its edge   // the pet in the tub (sitting height), its paws hidden by the tub
const TUB = { x: 230, w: 670 };                     // the tub reaches the shower pipe at x 900
const SHOWER_HOME = [858, 232];                     // where the shower head hangs
const SPRAY = [-.6, .8];                            // which way the water sprays from the head (down and to the left)
const BATH_STEPS = [
  { id: 'soap',  name: 'Soap',  say: n => `Rub the soap on ${n}.`, ok: n => '' },
  { id: 'rinse', name: 'Rinse', say: n => `Rinse off the bubbles.`, ok: n => '' },
  { id: 'towel', name: 'Dry',   say: n => `Dry ${n} with the towel.`, ok: n => '' },
  { id: 'brush', name: 'Brush', say: n => `Brush ${n}'s fur.`, ok: n => '' },
];
const GROOM_STEPS = [   // cats: brush, then trim the nails (no tub, no water)
  { id: 'brush', name: 'Brush', say: n => `Brush ${n}'s fur.`, ok: n => '' },
  { id: 'nails', name: 'Nails', say: n => `Trim ${n}'s nails. Snip each front paw.`, ok: n => '' },
];
const STEPS = () => kind === 'cat' ? GROOM_STEPS : BATH_STEPS;
const RUB_DONE = .7;   // how much of the pet you have to rub
const RUB_MIN_MS = 3000;   // and for at least 3 seconds; the step only finishes when you let go of the tool (Mac, Nov 26, 2026)
let bt = null;

const PAWS = {};   // filled in by build.py: each pose picture's paw width and paw middle, in picture pixels
// Where each pose sits on its sitting picture (x, y, width, height, in the sitting picture's 443 x 560 units), measured so the
// body lines up with the sitting picture: pets don't slide sideways or grow and shrink when they change pose.
// New pet: run python3 vet-clinic/pose_fit.py <id> and paste its lines here (it also makes a check sheet).
const FIT = {"ralphieBath": [-16, -20, 464, 580], "ralphieDone": [-4, 0, 452, 560], "bellaBath": [-4, -32, 473, 592], "bellaDone": [-4, -12, 461, 571], "zuzuBath": [-16, -4, 450, 563], "zuzuDone": [-4, 0, 452, 560], "spudBath": [-40, -36, 478, 597], "spudDone": [-4, 0, 452, 560], "millyBath": [-12, -16, 459, 574], "millyDone": [-4, 0, 452, 560], "suzyBath": [-8, 0, 450, 563], "suzyDone": [-4, 0, 452, 560], "willowBath": [0, -16, 459, 574], "willowDone": [0, 12, 443, 549], "roxyBath": [-32, -16, 469, 586], "roxyDone": [-24, 4, 452, 560], "griffinBath": [-4, 4, 445, 557], "griffinDone": [-4, 0, 452, 560], "lunaGroom": [-4, -4, 450, 563], "lunaDone": [-4, 0, 452, 560], "lunaPlay": [-4, 0, 450, 563], "peachesGroom": [-4, -4, 450, 563], "peachesDone": [-4, 0, 452, 560], "felixGroom": [4, 4, 445, 557], "felixDone": [0, 4, 447, 554], "felixPlay": [8, -60, 496, 621], "tuckerGroom": [-4, -4, 450, 563], "tuckerDone": [-4, 0, 452, 560], "tuckerPlay": [-4, -4, 450, 563], "finnGroom": [-4, 4, 445, 557], "finnDone": [-4, 0, 452, 560], "finnPlay": [-4, -4, 450, 563], "misoGroom": [-4, 0, 450, 563], "misoDone": [-4, 0, 452, 560], "misoPlay": [-4, 0, 450, 563], "jaxGroom": [-4, -4, 450, 563], "jaxDone": [-12, 4, 443, 549], "jaxPlay": [-60, -60, 492, 615], "finleyGroom": [0, 4, 445, 557], "finleyDone": [0, 12, 443, 549], "finleyPlay": [0, 4, 445, 557]};
function poseBox(pose, cx, bottom, sitH, byCollar) {
  const P = POSES[pose], k = sitH / 560, a = PAWS[(pose === 'playDown' ? POSES.play : P).art(pet)], s = PAWS[pet.art];
  const fit = FIT[P.art(pet)];
  if (fit && !(pet.playFit && (pose === 'play' || pose === 'playDown'))) {   // measured: lined up with the sitting picture
    const sx = cx - POSES.sit.w * k / 2, sy = bottom - 560 * k;
    return { x: sx + fit[0] * k, y: sy + fit[1] * k, w: fit[2] * k, h: fit[3] * k };
  }
  if (pet.playFit && (pose === 'play' || pose === 'playDown')) {   // a play picture turned to the side: placed where it sits on the sitting picture
    const [fx, fy, fw, fh] = pet.playFit, sx = cx - POSES.sit.w * k / 2, sy = bottom - 560 * k;
    return { x: sx + fx * k, y: sy + fy * k, w: fw * k, h: fh * k };
  }
  if (!a || !s) return { x: cx - P.w * k / 2 + P.dx * k, y: bottom - P.h * k, w: P.w * k, h: P.h * k };
  // every pose is drawn so its paws are as wide as when sitting
  const f = s[0] / a[0], sx = cx - POSES.sit.w * k / 2, sy = bottom - 560 * k;   // where the sitting picture's corner is
  // in the tub (paws hidden) the collar stays in the same spot, so the head does not jump; on the floor the paws do
  if (byCollar) return { x: sx + s[2] * k - a[2] * k * f, y: sy + s[3] * k - a[3] * k * f, w: P.w * k * f, h: P.h * k * f };
  const mid = sx + s[1] * k;
  return { x: mid - a[1] * k * f, y: bottom - P.h * k * f, w: P.w * k * f, h: P.h * k * f };
}
function poseImg(pose) {
  const b = poseBox(pose, TUB_DOG.cx, tubBottom(), tubH(), kind !== 'cat');   // a cat's paws show, so they stay put
  return `<image id="btDog" href="${ART[POSES[pose].art(pet)]}" x="${b.x}" y="${b.y}" width="${b.w}" height="${b.h}"/>`;
}

// which parts of the pet can be rubbed: a grid over the picture, keeping only squares where the pet is
function petCells(artKey, box, cols, rows) {
  return new Promise(res => {
    const im = new Image();
    im.onload = () => {
      const c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = 60; c.height = 76;
      const g = c.getContext('2d'); g.drawImage(im, 0, 0, 60, 76);
      const d = g.getImageData(0, 0, 60, 76).data, cells = [];
      for (let r = 0; r < rows; r++) for (let q = 0; q < cols; q++) {
        const px = Math.floor((q + .5) / cols * 60), py = Math.floor((r + .5) / rows * 76);
        if (d[(py * 60 + px) * 4 + 3] > 200 && r < rows * .75)   // the bottom part is inside the tub
          cells.push({ x: box.x + (q + .5) / cols * box.w, y: box.y + (r + .5) / rows * box.h, done: false });
      }
      res(cells);
    };
    im.src = ART[artKey];
  });
}

function bathStepsHtml() {
  const icons = { soap: 'bubbles', rinse: 'drop', towel: 'towel', brush: 'brush', nails: 'clip' };
  return STEPS().map((s, i) => {
    const done = i < bt.step, cur = i === bt.step, next = bt.ready && i === bt.step + 1;
    return `<div class="task${cur ? ' cur' : ''}${done ? ' done' : ''}${next ? ' blink' : ''}" data-i="${i}"${next ? ' role="button" tabindex="0"' : ''}>${icon(done ? 'check' : icons[s.id])}<span>${s.name}</span></div>`;
  }).join('');
}

function tubSvg() {
  return `<g><rect x="${TUB.x}" y="360" width="${TUB.w}" height="40" rx="20" fill="#dfe7ee"/>
    <rect x="${TUB.x}" y="380" width="${TUB.w}" height="140" rx="44" fill="#f1f5f8"/>
    <rect x="${TUB.x + 40}" y="404" width="${TUB.w - 80}" height="12" rx="6" fill="#ffffff" opacity=".7"/></g>`;
}
function stinkSvg(box) {   // three wavy "stinky" lines rising beside the pet's head
  const top = box.y + box.h * .12, l = box.x + box.w * .05, r = box.x + box.w * .95;
  const wave = (x, y) => `<path class="stink" d="M${x} ${y} q-14 -18 0 -36 q14 -18 0 -36 q-14 -18 0 -36" fill="none" stroke="#9cb36b" stroke-width="7" stroke-linecap="round"/>`;
  return `<g id="btStink">${wave(l - 20, top + 60)}${wave(l + 30, top + 10)}${wave(r - 10, top + 30)}${wave(r + 30, top + 80)}</g>`;
}
function showerSvg() {
  // the shower: a pipe on the tub's right edge, a hose, and a hand shower you can pick up
  return `<rect x="900" y="110" width="22" height="290" rx="11" fill="#b7c0ca"/>
    <rect x="884" y="102" width="54" height="22" rx="11" fill="#9aa6b2"/>
    <path id="btHose" d="" fill="none" stroke="#a7b2bd" stroke-width="10" stroke-linecap="round"/>
    <g class="bt-tool bt-head" id="btHead" data-tool="rinse" role="button" tabindex="0" aria-label="Shower, move it over the pet to rinse">
      <circle class="ring" cx="40" cy="-50" r="105" fill="rgba(255,246,176,.18)" stroke="#fff6b0" stroke-width="12"/>
      <path d="M8 -12 L88 -122" stroke="#c9d3dc" stroke-width="26" stroke-linecap="round"/>
      <path d="M14 -20 L80 -110" stroke="#e8eef3" stroke-width="7" stroke-linecap="round"/>
      <ellipse cx="0" cy="0" rx="44" ry="24" fill="#b7c0ca" transform="rotate(37)"/>
      <ellipse cx="-6" cy="8" rx="34" ry="11" fill="#e3e9ee" transform="rotate(37)"/>
      <g class="spray">${[0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8].map(i => {
        const side = (i % 3 - 1) * 16, sx = -6 + side * .8, sy = 12 + side * .6;
        return `<rect class="rain" x="${sx}" y="${sy}" width="7" height="22" rx="3.5" fill="#8fd0f5" style="animation-delay:${i * .05}s"/>`;
      }).join('')}</g>
    </g>`;
}
function setHead(x, y) {
  bt.head = [x, y];
  document.getElementById('btHead').setAttribute('transform', `translate(${x} ${y})`);
  const ex = x + 92, ey = y - 128;   // the end of the handle
  document.getElementById('btHose').setAttribute('d', `M911 398 C 990 470, ${ex + 120} ${ey + 60}, ${ex} ${ey}`);
}
function toolSvg(id, x, y, w) {
  const art = { soap: ART.soap, towel: ART.towel, brush: ART.brush, nails: ART.nails }[id], h = w * TOOL_SIZE[id];
  return `<g class="bt-tool" id="bt-${id}" data-tool="${id}" role="button" tabindex="0" aria-label="${id}">
    ${ringSvg(x - 14, y - 14, w + 28, h + 28)}<image href="${art}" x="${x}" y="${y}" width="${w}" height="${h}"/></g>`;
}
ART.nails = 'data:image/svg+xml,' + encodeURIComponent(`<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 200 110">
  <path d="M74 50 L180 20" stroke="#f28fb0" stroke-width="26" stroke-linecap="round"/><path d="M74 60 L180 90" stroke="#e0457b" stroke-width="26" stroke-linecap="round"/>
  <path d="M96 40 L168 20" stroke="#f7b6cc" stroke-width="7" stroke-linecap="round"/>
  <path d="M14 48 Q4 55 14 62 L80 62 L80 48 Z" fill="#b7c0ca"/><path d="M20 51 L72 51" stroke="#e8eef3" stroke-width="5" stroke-linecap="round"/>
  <circle cx="80" cy="55" r="13" fill="#9aa6b2"/><circle cx="77" cy="52" r="4" fill="#e8eef3"/></svg>`);   // nail clippers
const TOOL_SIZE = { soap: 0, towel: 0, brush: 0, nails: 0 };   // height / width of each tool picture, filled in below
['soap', 'towel', 'brush', 'nails'].forEach(k => { const im = new Image(); im.onload = () => { TOOL_SIZE[k] = im.height / im.width; }; im.src = ART[k]; });

const IN_TUB = { cx: 1760, bottom: 560, k: .52 };   // where the pet sits in the room's tub: inside it, behind the front wall, seen through the step-in opening (Mac, Nov 25, 2026)
const TUB_FRONT = 'M1600 340 L1632 345 L1672 500 L1695 500 L1695 585 L1815 585 L1815 500 L1975 500 L1975 700 L1600 700 Z';   // the tub's left side and front wall (with its step-in opening), drawn over the pet
function goToBath() {
  if (busy || !TASKS[task] || !['bubbles', 'brush'].includes(TASKS[task].icon)) return;
  busy = true;
  document.querySelector('.petg').classList.remove('drag');
  room.querySelector('[data-t="bath"]').classList.remove('on');
  guideEl.classList.add('hide');
  petMove(IN_TUB.cx - RUG.cx, IN_TUB.bottom - RUG.bottom, IN_TUB.k, 750);   // into the tub
  setTimeout(() => { document.getElementById('tubFront').style.opacity = 1; }, 650);
  setTimeout(() => { busy = false; openBath(); }, 1150);   // the bath screen opens once the pet is in
}
async function openBath() {
  busy = true;
  bt = { step: 0, ready: false, cells: [], drag: null, bubbles: {} };
  const box = poseBox('sit', TUB_DOG.cx, tubBottom(), tubH());
  bt.box = box;
  bt.cells = await petCells(pet.art, box, 7, 8);
  const cat = kind === 'cat';
  document.getElementById('btTitle').textContent = cat ? `Groom ${pet.name}` : `${pet.name}'s bath`;
  document.getElementById('btStage').innerHTML = cat ? `
    <rect x="0" y="0" width="1100" height="520" fill="#fffaf5" rx="20"/>
    <ellipse cx="${TUB_DOG.cx}" cy="${tubBottom() - 4}" rx="290" ry="44" fill="#e6dcf2"/><ellipse cx="${TUB_DOG.cx}" cy="${tubBottom() - 8}" rx="240" ry="30" fill="#f1ebf8"/>
    <g id="btDogLayer">${poseImg('sit')}<g id="btFx"></g></g>
    ${toolSvg('brush', 50, 110, 160)}${toolSvg('nails', 40, 300, 180)}
    <g id="btTop"></g>` : `
    <rect x="0" y="0" width="1100" height="520" fill="#fffaf5" rx="20"/>
    <g id="btDogLayer">${poseImg('sit')}${stinkSvg(box)}<g id="btFx"></g></g>
    ${tubSvg()}
    ${showerSvg()}
    ${toolSvg('soap', 40, 70, 150)}${toolSvg('towel', 30, 220, 170)}${toolSvg('brush', 40, 370, 160)}
    <g id="btTop"></g>`;
  if (!cat) setHead(...SHOWER_HOME);
  openOverlay(document.getElementById('bath'));
  bathStep();
  setTimeout(() => { petMove(0, 0, 1, 0); document.getElementById('tubFront').style.opacity = 0; }, 400);   // behind the bath screen, back to the rug
}

function bathStep() {
  const s = STEPS()[bt.step], next = STEPS()[bt.step + 1];
  document.getElementById('btSteps').innerHTML = bathStepsHtml();
  document.getElementById('btSay').textContent = !s ? '' : bt.ready ? s.ok(pet.name) : s.say(pet.name);
  document.querySelectorAll('.bt-tool').forEach(g => {
    const i = STEPS().findIndex(x => x.id === g.dataset.tool);
    g.classList.toggle('on', !!s && ((i === bt.step && !bt.ready) || (bt.ready && i === bt.step + 1)));
    g.classList.toggle('used', i < bt.step);
  });
}
function nextBathStep() {
  if (!bt.ready || bt.step >= STEPS().length - 1) return;
  bt.step++; bt.ready = false; bt.rubT0 = 0;
  bt.cells.forEach(c => c.done = false);
  document.querySelectorAll('#btFx .puff').forEach(p => p.remove());
  if (STEPS()[bt.step].id === 'nails') nailSpots();
  if (STEPS()[bt.step].id === 'towel') document.querySelectorAll('#btFx .bub').forEach(b => b.remove());
  bathStep();
}
document.getElementById('btSteps').addEventListener('click', e => { if (e.target.closest('.task.blink')) nextBathStep(); });

const btStage = document.getElementById('btStage');
function btPoint(e) {
  const p = btStage.createSVGPoint(); p.x = e.clientX; p.y = e.clientY;
  return p.matrixTransform(btStage.getScreenCTM().inverse());
}
btStage.addEventListener('pointerdown', e => {
  const t = e.target.closest('.bt-tool');
  if (!bt || !t) return;
  const i = STEPS().findIndex(x => x.id === t.dataset.tool);
  if (bt.ready && i === bt.step + 1) nextBathStep();   // picking up the next tool moves on
  if (i !== bt.step) return;
  e.preventDefault();
  const p = btPoint(e);
  if (t.id === 'btHead') {
    bt.drag = { head: true, ox: p.x - bt.head[0], oy: p.y - bt.head[1] };
    t.classList.add('raining');
  } else {
    const img = t.querySelector('image');
    bt.drag = { el: t, img, ox: p.x - +img.getAttribute('x'), oy: p.y - +img.getAttribute('y'), home: [img.getAttribute('x'), img.getAttribute('y')] };
    document.getElementById('btTop').appendChild(t);   // carry it over the pet
  }
  t.querySelector('.ring').style.display = 'none';
  btStage.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
});
btStage.addEventListener('pointermove', e => {
  if (!bt || !bt.drag) return;
  const p = btPoint(e), d = bt.drag;
  if (d.head) {   // the shower sprays down and to the left of the head
    setHead(p.x - d.ox, p.y - d.oy);
    return rubAt(bt.head[0] + SPRAY[0] * 120, bt.head[1] + SPRAY[1] * 120, 70);
  }
  const x = p.x - d.ox, y = p.y - d.oy;
  d.img.setAttribute('x', x); d.img.setAttribute('y', y);
  if (d.el.dataset.tool === 'brush') {   // turn the bristles toward the pet
    const w = +d.img.getAttribute('width'), h = +d.img.getAttribute('height'), cx = x + w * .3, cy = y + h * .35;
    const dogX = bt.box.x + bt.box.w / 2, dogY = bt.box.y + bt.box.h * .4;
    const a = (Math.atan2(dogY - cy, dogX - cx) * 180 / Math.PI + 90) * .95;   // the bristles point up in the picture (turned 5% less)
    d.img.setAttribute('transform', `rotate(${a} ${cx} ${cy})`);
  }
  rubAt(p.x, p.y, 46);
});
function dropTool() {
  if (!bt || !bt.drag) return;
  const d = bt.drag; bt.drag = null;
  rubCheck();
  if (d.head) {
    document.getElementById('btHead').classList.remove('raining');
    document.querySelector('#btHead .ring').style.display = '';
    return setHead(...SHOWER_HOME);   // back on its hook
  }
  d.img.setAttribute('x', d.home[0]); d.img.setAttribute('y', d.home[1]); d.img.removeAttribute('transform');
  d.el.querySelector('.ring').style.display = '';
}
btStage.addEventListener('pointerup', dropTool);
btStage.addEventListener('pointercancel', dropTool);

function rubAt(x, y, reach) {
  const step = STEPS()[bt.step].id, fx = document.getElementById('btFx');
  let hit = false;
  if (!bt.rubT0) bt.rubT0 = Date.now();   // when this step's rubbing started
  bt.cells.forEach((c, i) => {
    if (c.done || Math.hypot(c.x - x, c.y - y) > reach) return;
    c.done = true; hit = true;
    if (step === 'soap') {
      const jx = (i * 37) % 23 - 11, jy = (i * 53) % 19 - 9, r = 15 + (i * 7) % 9;   // a little uneven, like real bubbles
      fx.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', `<g class="bub" data-cell="${i}"><circle cx="${c.x - 8 + jx}" cy="${c.y + jy}" r="${r}" fill="#fff" opacity=".92"/><circle cx="${c.x + 12 + jx}" cy="${c.y - 12 + jy}" r="${r * .6}" fill="#fff" opacity=".92"/><circle cx="${c.x - 12 + jx}" cy="${c.y - 6 + jy}" r="4" fill="#d7eefc"/></g>`);
    } else if (step === 'nails') {   // snip: the paw's glow goes and a little star pops
      fx.querySelectorAll(`.nail-spot[data-cell="${i}"]`).forEach(n => n.remove());
      fx.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', `<polygon class="snip" points="${starPts(c.x, c.y - 6, 26, 11)}" fill="#ffd34d"/>` + [-1, 0, 1].map(k =>
        `<path class="clipping" style="--dx:${k * 26}px;animation-delay:${(k + 1) * 140}ms" d="M${c.x + k * 12 - 7} ${c.y + 4} q7 -7 14 0" fill="none" stroke="#fffaf0" stroke-width="5" stroke-linecap="round"/>`).join(''));
      const cl = bt.drag && bt.drag.img; if (cl) { cl.classList.remove('clipping-tool'); void cl.getBBox(); cl.classList.add('clipping-tool'); }   // snip, snip
      if (window.tbBeep) [0, .22, .44].forEach(d => tbBeep([[2400, d, .025], [1500, d + .03, .03]], 'square'));
    } else if (step === 'rinse') {
      fx.querySelectorAll(`.bub[data-cell="${i}"]`).forEach(b => { b.style.opacity = 0; setTimeout(() => b.remove(), 400); });
    } else {
      const col = step === 'towel' ? '#bae5ca' : '#fff6b0';
      fx.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', `<circle class="puff" cx="${c.x}" cy="${c.y}" r="16" fill="${col}"/>`);
    }
  });
  if (!hit) return;
  const part = bt.cells.filter(c => c.done).length / bt.cells.length;
  if (step === 'soap') { const st = document.getElementById('btStink'); if (st) st.style.opacity = Math.max(0, 1 - part / RUB_DONE); }
  if (!bt.ready && step === 'nails' && part >= 1) stepReady(step);   // snipping: every paw, right away
}
// rubbing steps finish when you let go, after at least 3 s of rubbing and 70% of the pet (never cut off mid-rub)
function rubCheck() {
  const s = STEPS()[bt.step];
  if (!s || bt.ready || s.id === 'nails' || !bt.rubT0) return;
  const part = bt.cells.filter(c => c.done).length / bt.cells.length;
  if (part >= RUB_DONE && Date.now() - bt.rubT0 >= RUB_MIN_MS) stepReady(s.id);
}

function stepReady(step) {
  bt.ready = true;
  if (step === 'soap') { const st = document.getElementById('btStink'); if (st) st.remove(); }
  if (step === 'rinse') setDogPose('bath');
  if (step === 'towel') setDogPose('sit');
  if (step === 'nails') return setTimeout(groomDone, 1500);   // a moment to see both paws snipped
  if (step === 'brush' && kind === 'cat') { setDogPose('bath'); return bathStep(); }   // happy and fluffy; the clippers blink next
  if (step === 'brush') { bathStep(); return setTimeout(bathDone, 200); }   // you already let go: all clean
  bathStep();
}
function setDogPose(pose) {
  document.getElementById('btDog').outerHTML = poseImg(pose);
}

function bathDone() {
  bt.step = STEPS().length; bt.ready = false; bathStep();
  setDogPose('done');
  document.getElementById('btSay').textContent = `${pet.name} is all clean.`;
  const b = poseBox('done', TUB_DOG.cx, tubBottom(), tubH(), true), cx = b.x + b.w / 2, cy = b.y + b.h * .35;
  let stars = '';
  for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) {   // one small burst of stars, once
    const a = i / 8 * Math.PI * 2;
    stars += `<polygon points="${starPts(cx, cy, 22, 9)}" fill="#ffd34d" style="--bx:${Math.cos(a) * 190}px;--by:${Math.sin(a) * 150}px"/>`;
  }
  document.getElementById('btFx').innerHTML = `<g class="burst">${stars}</g>`;
  setTimeout(() => {
    closeOverlay(document.getElementById('bath'));
    // back on the rug, happy
    const img = document.querySelector('.petg image'), rb = poseBox('done', RUG.cx, RUG.bottom, rugH());
    img.setAttribute('href', ART[pet.done]); img.setAttribute('x', rb.x); img.setAttribute('y', rb.y); img.setAttribute('width', rb.w); img.setAttribute('height', rb.h);
    busy = false; setTask(4);
    document.getElementById('adTitle').textContent = 'Thanks for your help!';
    const paid = pet.name; setTimeout(() => payMe(10, `Helped ${paid} at the Vet Clinic`), 700);   // the All Done card says thanks and pays you
  }, 1600);
}
// cats: two glowing front paws to snip with the clippers
function nailSpots() {
  const b = poseBox('sit', TUB_DOG.cx, tubBottom(), tubH()), sp = PAWS[pet.art], k = b.w / POSES.sit.w;
  let mid = sp ? b.x + sp[1] * k : b.x + b.w / 2, pw = sp ? sp[0] * k : b.w * .6, y = b.y + b.h * .93, f = .24;
  // cats: the curled tail sits beside the paws, so the paws' middle is under the collar (the middle of the chest)
  if (kind === 'cat') { mid = sp ? b.x + sp[2] * k : mid; pw = b.w; f = .104; y = b.y + b.h * .92; }
  bt.cells = [-f, f].map(d => ({ x: mid + d * pw, y, done: false }));
  document.getElementById('btFx').insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', bt.cells.map((c, i) =>
    `<circle class="nail-spot" data-cell="${i}" cx="${c.x}" cy="${c.y}" r="28" fill="rgba(255,211,77,.3)" stroke="#ffc94d" stroke-width="7"/>`).join(''));
}
function starBurst(cx, cy, el) {   // one small burst of stars, once
  let stars = '';
  for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) { const a = i / 8 * Math.PI * 2;
    stars += `<polygon points="${starPts(cx, cy, 22, 9)}" fill="#ffd34d" style="--bx:${Math.cos(a) * 190}px;--by:${Math.sin(a) * 150}px"/>`; }
  el.innerHTML = `<g class="burst">${stars}</g>`;
}
function groomDone() {
  bt.step = STEPS().length; bt.ready = false; bathStep();
  document.getElementById('btSay').textContent = `${pet.name} is all groomed.`;
  const b = poseBox('bath', TUB_DOG.cx, tubBottom(), tubH()); starBurst(b.x + b.w / 2, b.y + b.h * .35, document.getElementById('btFx'));
  setTimeout(() => {
    closeOverlay(document.getElementById('bath'));
    setPose('sit'); busy = false; setTask(task + 1); playTime();
  }, 1600);
}
function starPts(cx, cy, R, r) {
  let p = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) { const a = -Math.PI / 2 + i * Math.PI / 5, rr = i % 2 ? r : R; p.push(`${cx + Math.cos(a) * rr},${cy + Math.sin(a) * rr}`); }
  return p.join(' ');
}
document.getElementById('btClose').addEventListener('click', () => { dropTool(); busy = false; closeOverlay(document.getElementById('bath')); setTask(task, true); });
document.getElementById('adAgain').addEventListener('click', () => {
  closeOverlay(document.getElementById('allDone'));
  clearPet(); TASKS = TASK_LISTS.dog; nameTarget('bath', 'Bath');
  setTask(0);
});

// a room spot's name can change (the Bath spot is called Groom for cats)
function nameTarget(id, name) {
  const g = room.querySelector(`[data-t="${id}"]`);
  g.querySelector('.tag').outerHTML = tagSvg(Object.assign({}, TARGETS[id], { name }));
  g.setAttribute('aria-label', name);
}

// ---------- Cat food: the cat's recipe, pick the right tool, scoop, then stir ----------
// Every visit the cat wants a new mix of dry and wet food. A picture checklist (top left) says what the cat needs.
// Drag the tsp or Tbsp straight to the food to scoop (the wrong spoon shakes and goes back), then to the bowl: it pours as soon as it gets there,
// and you can keep holding it and go back to the food for the next scoop. Then stir.
// The food and the bowl light up to show where to drag next, so you don't need to read.
const CF_TOOLS = {
  tsp:  { name: 'tsp',   x: 430, color: '#f08a24', light: '#ffc98f' },   // each spoon has its own handle color and a name badge, so you can tell them apart in your hand
  tbsp: { name: 'Tbsp',  x: 640, color: '#8a5cc7', light: '#c9b0ec' },
};
const CF_DRY = [ { tool: 'tbsp', n: 2 }, { tool: 'tbsp', n: 3 }, { tool: 'tbsp', n: 4 } ];
const CF_WET = [ { tool: 'tsp', n: 1 }, { tool: 'tsp', n: 2 }, { tool: 'tsp', n: 3 }, { tool: 'tbsp', n: 1 } ];
const CF_HOME_Y = 205, CF_HELD = [558, 290];   // the spoons' place (their name tags are above them)
const CF_BOWL = { x: 420, y: 340, w: 260 };
const CF_SRC = {   // the two open foods: where the tool dips in, where it waits once full, and the area you can let go on
  dry: { at: [929, 262], dip: [929, 296], rest: [929, 185], box: [818, 170, 236, 320] },
  wet: { at: [155, 320], dip: [155, 342], rest: [155, 250], box: [52, 282, 208, 208] },
};
const cfAmount = r => r.tool === 'cup' ? '¼ cup' : `${r.n} ${CF_TOOLS[r.tool].name}`;
const pickOne = a => a[Math.floor(Math.random() * a.length)];
const cfFood = () => {   // the food's top in the bowl: center and size
  const B = CF_BOWL, bh = B.w * 236 / 360;
  return { cx: B.x + B.w * BOWL_OPEN.cx, cy: B.y + bh * BOWL_OPEN.cy, rx: B.w * BOWL_OPEN.rx * .86, ry: bh * BOWL_OPEN.ry * .8, bh };
};
let cf = null;

function cfToolSvg(id) {
  const t = CF_TOOLS[id], hx = t.x, hy = CF_HOME_Y, ch = 140 * 307 / 360;
  const spoon = k => `<g transform="scale(${k})"><rect x="-8" y="-128" width="16" height="104" rx="8" fill="${t.color}"/><rect x="-3" y="-120" width="5" height="88" rx="2.5" fill="${t.light}"/>
      <ellipse cx="0" cy="0" rx="28" ry="34" fill="#c9d3dc"/><ellipse cx="-3" cy="-4" rx="20" ry="25" fill="#e8eef3"/>
      <ellipse class="cf-load" cx="0" cy="-2" rx="21" ry="24" fill="#b98557" opacity="0"/></g>
      <g transform="translate(0 ${-96 * k})"><rect x="-37" y="-18" width="74" height="36" rx="18" fill="${t.color}" stroke="#fff" stroke-width="3"/>
        <text y="8" text-anchor="middle" font-size="22" font-weight="bold" fill="#fff">${t.name}</text></g>`;
  const art = id === 'cup' ? `<image href="${ART.cup}" x="-70" y="-24" width="140" height="${ch}"/>
      <ellipse class="cf-load" cx="2" cy="${-24 + ch * .74}" rx="30" ry="11" fill="#b98557" opacity="0"/>` : spoon(id === 'tsp' ? .72 : 1);
  return `<g class="cf-tool" id="cf-${id}" data-tool="${id}" role="button" tabindex="0" aria-label="${t.name}">
    ${ringSvg(hx - 88, 2, 176, 258)}
    <g class="cf-tag"><rect x="${hx - 70}" y="10" width="140" height="46" rx="23" fill="${t.color}" stroke="#fff" stroke-width="4"/>
      <text x="${hx}" y="43" text-anchor="middle" font-size="27" font-weight="bold" fill="#fff">${t.name}</text></g>
    <clipPath id="cfDipClip-${id}"><rect x="${hx - 400}" y="-2000" width="800" height="${2000 + hy - 20}"/></clipPath>
    <g class="cf-move" style="transform-origin: ${hx}px ${hy}px"><g class="cf-wig"><g transform="translate(${hx} ${hy})">${art}</g></g></g></g>`;   // (the clip hides the tip while it is down in the food)
}
const cfLabel = (cx, y, text) => `<g class="cf-label"><rect x="${cx - 74}" y="${y}" width="148" height="40" rx="20" fill="#fff" stroke="#4a3b5c" stroke-width="4"/>
  <text x="${cx}" y="${y + 28}" text-anchor="middle" font-size="23" font-weight="bold" fill="#4a3b5c">${text}</text></g>`;
function cfBinSvg() {   // the dry food bin, open at the top (the same bin as the dogs' food)
  return `<g class="cf-src" id="cfDry" role="button" tabindex="0" aria-label="Dry food">${ringSvg(818, 170, 236, 320)}
    <g transform="translate(790 120) scale(.75)">
      <rect x="70" y="150" width="230" height="320" rx="34" fill="#e3eef0"/>
      <path d="M78 250 Q120 232 160 250 T240 248 T292 250 V440 Q292 462 270 462 H100 Q78 462 78 440 Z" fill="#b98557"/>
      ${[[120, 300], [190, 340], [245, 290], [150, 400], [230, 410]].map(([x, y]) => `<circle cx="${x}" cy="${y}" r="7" fill="#9a6a42"/>`).join('')}
      <rect x="92" y="170" width="22" height="270" rx="11" fill="#fff" opacity=".45"/>
      <rect x="64" y="140" width="242" height="22" rx="11" fill="#c4a690"/></g>
    ${cfLabel(929, 474, 'Dry food')}</g>`;
}
function cfCanSvg() {   // the wet food can, open, with a fish on its label
  return `<g class="cf-src" id="cfWet" role="button" tabindex="0" aria-label="Wet food">${ringSvg(52, 282, 208, 208)}
    <ellipse cx="226" cy="318" rx="44" ry="13" fill="#dfe7ee" transform="rotate(-28 226 318)"/>
    <rect x="75" y="332" width="160" height="134" rx="24" fill="#f28fb0"/>
    <rect x="75" y="364" width="160" height="66" fill="#fde3ec"/>
    <ellipse cx="148" cy="397" rx="30" ry="16" fill="#f7b6cc"/><path d="M174 397 L196 383 L196 411 Z" fill="#f7b6cc"/><circle cx="134" cy="393" r="4" fill="#e0457b"/>
    <ellipse cx="155" cy="334" rx="80" ry="20" fill="#c9d3dc"/><ellipse cx="155" cy="335" rx="68" ry="14" fill="#a0563f"/>
    <ellipse cx="138" cy="331" rx="22" ry="5" fill="#bb6f55"/>
    ${cfLabel(155, 474, 'Wet food')}</g>`;
}
function cfBowlSvg() {
  const B = CF_BOWL, f = cfFood(), bh = f.bh, { cx, cy, rx, ry } = f;
  return `<g class="cf-src" id="cfBowl" role="button" tabindex="0" aria-label="Food bowl">${ringSvg(B.x - 20, B.y - 20, B.w + 40, bh + 40)}
    <g id="cfBowlArt"><image href="${ART.bowlFood}" x="${B.x}" y="${B.y}" width="${B.w}" height="${bh}"/>
    <ellipse class="cf-food" id="cfDryIn" cx="${cx}" cy="${cy}" rx="${rx}" ry="${ry}" fill="#b98557" style="transform: scale(0)"/>
    <g id="cfKib"></g>
    <ellipse class="cf-food" id="cfWetIn" cx="${cx + 8}" cy="${cy - 3}" rx="${rx * .56}" ry="${ry * .62}" fill="#a0563f" style="transform: scale(0)"/>
    <g class="cf-food" id="cfMixIn" opacity="0"><ellipse cx="${cx}" cy="${cy}" rx="${rx}" ry="${ry}" fill="#a36b48"/>
      ${[[-50, -4], [-20, 6], [14, -6], [44, 4], [-4, -12], [30, 12]].map(([x, y], i) => `<circle cx="${cx + x}" cy="${cy + y}" r="${6 - i % 2}" fill="${i % 2 ? '#7f4a32' : '#c08a5e'}"/>`).join('')}</g></g></g>`;
}
function cfFrontSvg() {   // the near half of the bowl and food, drawn again over the spoon, so the spoon goes into the food
  const B = CF_BOWL, { cx, cy, rx, bh } = cfFood();
  return `<g id="cfFront" style="opacity: 0; pointer-events: none"><clipPath id="cfFrontClip">
    <path d="M${B.x - 10} ${cy} H${cx - rx} A${rx} ${cfFood().ry} 0 0 0 ${cx + rx} ${cy} H${B.x + B.w + 10} V${B.y + bh + 10} H${B.x - 10} Z"/></clipPath>
    <use href="#cfBowlArt" clip-path="url(#cfFrontClip)"/></g>`;
}
// the checklist: little pictures of the can, the bin and a spoon, the amount, and a dot for each scoop
const CF_MINI = {
  dry: `<rect x="-14" y="-14" width="28" height="30" rx="6" fill="#e3eef0" stroke="#c4a690" stroke-width="2"/><rect x="-12" y="-4" width="24" height="18" rx="4" fill="#b98557"/><rect x="-16" y="-17" width="32" height="5" rx="2.5" fill="#c4a690"/>`,
  wet: `<rect x="-15" y="-9" width="30" height="24" rx="6" fill="#f28fb0"/><rect x="-15" y="-2" width="30" height="10" fill="#fde3ec"/><ellipse cx="0" cy="-9" rx="15" ry="5" fill="#c9d3dc"/><ellipse cx="0" cy="-9" rx="12" ry="3.5" fill="#a0563f"/>`,
  stir: `<ellipse cx="0" cy="8" rx="17" ry="7" fill="#a36b48"/><path d="M-10 6 A10 4 0 1 0 10 6" stroke="#fff" stroke-width="2.5" fill="none" stroke-dasharray="4 3"/><rect x="2" y="-18" width="5" height="24" rx="2.5" fill="#9aa6b2" transform="rotate(20 4 0)"/>`,
};
function cfListSvg() {
  const c = cf, step = { pickDry: 0, scoopDry: 0, pickWet: 1, scoopWet: 1, stir: 2, done: 3 }[c.phase];
  const rows = [
    { mini: 'dry', name: 'Dry food', r: c.dry, count: step > 0 ? (c.dry.tool === 'cup' ? 1 : c.dry.n) : step === 0 ? c.count : 0 },
    { mini: 'wet', name: 'Wet food', r: c.wet, count: step > 1 ? c.wet.n : step === 1 ? c.count : 0 },
    { mini: 'stir', name: 'Stir' } ];
  let s = `<rect x="8" y="6" width="236" height="262" rx="22" fill="#fffdf3" stroke="#8a5a3b" stroke-width="4"/>
    <text class="cf-title" x="24" y="44" font-size="26" font-weight="bold" fill="#4a3b5c">${pet.name} needs:</text>`;
  rows.forEach((row, i) => {
    const y = 64 + i * 66, done = i < step, cur = i === step;
    if (cur) s += `<rect x="16" y="${y}" width="220" height="60" rx="14" fill="#fff1a8" stroke="#ffc94d" stroke-width="3"/>`;
    s += done ? `<g transform="translate(36 ${y + 30}) scale(.62)">${ICONS.check}</g>` : `<circle cx="36" cy="${y + 30}" r="6" fill="#6b4329"/>`;
    s += `<g transform="translate(70 ${y + 30})" opacity="${done ? .55 : 1}">${CF_MINI[row.mini]}</g>`;
    const col = done ? '#8d8597' : '#4a3b5c';
    if (!row.r) { s += `<text x="96" y="${y + 39}" font-size="24" font-weight="bold" fill="${col}">${row.name}</text>`; return; }
    const n = row.r.tool === 'cup' ? 1 : row.r.n;
    s += `<text x="96" y="${y + 25}" font-size="20" font-weight="bold" fill="${col}">${row.name}</text>
      <text class="cf-amt" x="96" y="${y + 51}" font-size="22" font-weight="bold" fill="${done ? col : row.r.tool === 'cup' ? '#b8336a' : CF_TOOLS[row.r.tool].color}">${cfAmount(row.r)}</text><g class="cf-dots">`;
