    if (n > 1) for (let k = 0; k < n; k++)   // a dot for each scoop, filled in as you scoop (moved to just after the amount below)
      s += `<circle cx="${k * 13}" cy="${y + 44}" r="5" fill="${k < row.count ? '#5fae7a' : '#fff'}" stroke="${k < row.count ? '#5fae7a' : '#b9a99a'}" stroke-width="2.5"/>`;
    s += '</g>';
  });
  return s;
}
function cfArrow(from, to) {   // (no arrow any more: the food and bowl light up instead)
  const h = document.getElementById('cfHint');
  h.innerHTML = ''; return;
  if (!from) { h.innerHTML = ''; return; }
  const [x1, y1] = from, [x2, y2] = to, mx = (x1 + x2) / 2, my = Math.min(y1, y2) - 90;
  const a = Math.atan2(y2 - my, x2 - mx), back = 22, hx = x2 - Math.cos(a) * 8, hy = y2 - Math.sin(a) * 8;
  const head = [[hx, hy], [hx - Math.cos(a - .5) * back, hy - Math.sin(a - .5) * back], [hx - Math.cos(a + .5) * back, hy - Math.sin(a + .5) * back]];
  h.innerHTML = `<path class="cf-arrow" d="M${x1} ${y1} Q${mx} ${my} ${hx - Math.cos(a) * 14} ${hy - Math.sin(a) * 14}" fill="none" stroke="#e0457b" stroke-width="7" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-dasharray="14 12"/>
    <polygon points="${head.map(p => p.join(',')).join(' ')}" fill="#e0457b"/>`;
}

function openCatFood() {
  hideExtra('shelfFood');   // the bowl comes off the shelf
  ms = null;                // (the dogs' measuring cup is not used)
  if (!pet.recipe) pet.recipe = { dry: pickOne(CF_DRY), wet: pickOne(CF_WET) };   // what this cat wants today
  cf = { dry: pet.recipe.dry, wet: pet.recipe.wet, phase: 'pickDry', held: null, count: 0, mix: 0, anim: false, stir: null, loaded: false, hint: null, pos: null, drag: null, wrong: 0, stuck: null };
  document.getElementById('msTitle').textContent = `Make ${pet.name}'s food`;
  document.getElementById('msSay').style.display = 'none';   // no sentences: the checklist and arrows show what to do
  document.getElementById('msStage').innerHTML = cfCanSvg() + cfBinSvg() + cfBowlSvg() + Object.keys(CF_TOOLS).map(cfToolSvg).join('')
    + cfFrontSvg() + '<g id="cfHint" style="pointer-events: none"></g><g id="cfList" transform="translate(4 4) scale(.88)" style="pointer-events: none"></g>';
  msView(MS_VIEW.full); msButtons(null);
  openOverlay(document.getElementById('measure'));
  cfUpdate(); cfStuckTimer();
}
function cfStuckTimer() {   // no right pick after 10 seconds: the right tool lights up
  const c = cf; clearTimeout(c.stuck);
  c.stuck = setTimeout(() => { if (cf === c && (c.phase === 'pickDry' || c.phase === 'pickWet')) { c.hint = (c.phase === 'pickDry' ? c.dry : c.wet).tool; cfUpdate(); } }, 10000);
}
const cfSrcKey = () => cf.phase === 'scoopDry' ? 'dry' : cf.phase === 'scoopWet' ? 'wet' : null;
function cfUpdate() {   // the checklist, which things glow, and where the arrow points
  const c = cf, picking = c.phase === 'pickDry' || c.phase === 'pickWet', k = cfSrcKey();
  document.getElementById('cfList').innerHTML = cfListSvg();
  const title = document.querySelector('#cfList .cf-title'), tw = title.getComputedTextLength();
  if (tw > 204) title.setAttribute('font-size', Math.floor(26 * 204 / tw));   // a long name: smaller letters, so it stays inside the card
  document.querySelectorAll('#cfList .cf-amt').forEach(t => t.nextElementSibling.setAttribute('transform', `translate(${96 + t.getComputedTextLength() + 11} 0)`));
  document.querySelectorAll('.cf-tool').forEach(g => {
    const hint = picking && c.hint === g.dataset.tool;   // no glow on the tools unless you are stuck
    g.classList.toggle('on', hint); g.classList.toggle('hint', hint);
    g.classList.toggle('cf-held', g.dataset.tool === c.held);
  });
  document.getElementById('cfDry').classList.toggle('on', k === 'dry' && !c.loaded);
  document.getElementById('cfWet').classList.toggle('on', k === 'wet' && !c.loaded);
  document.getElementById('cfBowl').classList.toggle('on', (k && c.loaded) || (c.phase === 'stir' && c.mix === 0 && !c.stir));
  document.getElementById('cfFront').style.opacity = c.phase === 'stir' ? 1 : 0;
  document.querySelectorAll('.cf-tool').forEach(g => {   // stirring: the spoon's tip is down in the food, wherever it is
    const w = g.querySelector('.cf-wig'), dip = c.phase === 'stir' && g.dataset.tool === c.held;
    if (dip) w.setAttribute('clip-path', `url(#cfDipClip-${g.dataset.tool})`); else w.removeAttribute('clip-path'); });
  if (c.anim || c.drag || c.stir) return cfArrow(null);
  if (k && !c.loaded) cfArrow([c.pos[0], c.pos[1] - 40], [CF_SRC[k].at[0], CF_SRC[k].at[1] - 20]);
  else if (k) cfArrow([c.pos[0], c.pos[1] - 40], [CF_BOWL.x + 130, CF_BOWL.y + 10]);
  else cfArrow(null);
}
function cfMove(id, x, y, rot, ms_) {
  const t = CF_TOOLS[id], el = document.querySelector(`#cf-${id} .cf-move`);
  el.style.transition = ms_ ? `transform ${ms_}ms ease-in-out` : 'none';
  el.style.transform = `translate(${x - t.x}px, ${y - CF_HOME_Y}px) rotate(${rot}deg)`;
  if (cf && id === cf.held) cf.pos = [x, y];
}
function cfLoad(id, wet, on) {
  const l = document.querySelector(`#cf-${id} .cf-load`);
  l.setAttribute('fill', wet ? '#a0563f' : '#b98557'); l.setAttribute('opacity', on ? 1 : 0);
}
function cfPickTool(id) {
  const c = cf, dry = c.phase === 'pickDry';
  if (c.anim || !(dry || c.phase === 'pickWet')) return;
  const r = dry ? c.dry : c.wet;
  if (id !== r.tool) {   // not this one: it shakes; after two wrong picks the right one lights up
    const w = document.querySelector(`#cf-${id} .cf-wig`);
    w.classList.remove('shake'); void w.getBBox(); w.classList.add('shake');
    if (++c.wrong >= 2) { c.hint = r.tool; cfUpdate(); }
    return;
  }
  clearTimeout(c.stuck); c.wrong = 0;
  c.held = id; c.count = 0; c.hint = null; c.loaded = false; c.phase = dry ? 'scoopDry' : 'scoopWet';
  cfMove(id, ...CF_HELD, 0, 450);
  c.anim = true; cfUpdate();
  setTimeout(() => { c.anim = false; cfUpdate(); }, 460);
}
function cfDip() {   // into the open food: down, a little scoop, and back up full
  const c = cf, k = cfSrcKey(), S = CF_SRC[k], id = c.held;
  c.anim = true; cfUpdate();
  cfMove(id, ...S.at, 0, 300);
  setTimeout(() => cfMove(id, ...S.dip, 18, 260), 320);
  setTimeout(() => { cfLoad(id, k === 'wet', true); cfMove(id, ...S.rest, 0, 320); }, 620);
  setTimeout(() => { c.anim = false; c.loaded = true; cfUpdate(); }, 960);
}
function cfTip() {   // over the bowl, tip it in
  const c = cf, wet = cfSrcKey() === 'wet', r = wet ? c.wet : c.dry, id = c.held, n = r.tool === 'cup' ? 1 : r.n;
  c.anim = true; cfUpdate();
  cfMove(id, CF_BOWL.x + 150, CF_BOWL.y - 30, 0, 300);
  setTimeout(() => cfMove(id, CF_BOWL.x + 160, CF_BOWL.y - 10, -60, 220), 320);
  setTimeout(() => {
    cfPour(c, id, wet);
    cfMove(id, ...CF_HELD, 0, 260);
    setTimeout(() => { c.anim = false; if (c.count >= n) cfScoopsDone(c, id, wet); cfUpdate(); }, 280);
  }, 560);
}
function cfPour(c, id, wet) {   // the spoon empties into the bowl, and the food in the bowl grows
  const r = wet ? c.wet : c.dry, n = r.tool === 'cup' ? 1 : r.n;
  cfLoad(id, wet, false); c.count++; c.loaded = false;
  const k = Math.min(1, .45 + .55 * c.count / n);
  document.getElementById(wet ? 'cfWetIn' : 'cfDryIn').style.transform = `scale(${k})`;
  if (!wet) document.getElementById('cfKib').insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', [0, 1, 2].map(i => {
    const f = cfFood(), x = f.cx + ((c.count * 37 + i * 29) % 90 - 45) * k, y = f.cy + ((c.count * 13 + i * 11) % 20 - 10) * k;
    return `<circle cx="${x}" cy="${y}" r="6" fill="#9a6a42"/>`; }).join(''));
}
function cfScoopsDone(c, id, wet) {   // all the scoops are in: dry food's spoon goes back in its place, wet food's goes down into the food to stir
  if (!wet) { c.phase = 'pickWet'; c.held = null; c.count = 0; cfMove(id, CF_TOOLS[id].x, CF_HOME_Y, 0, 420); cfStuckTimer(); }
  else { c.phase = 'stir'; cfMove(id, ...cfStirAt(0, 0), 0, 400); }
}
function cfTipHeld(d) {   // a full spoon held over the bowl: it tips in right where it is, and you keep holding it to go back for more
  const c = cf, wet = cfSrcKey() === 'wet', r = wet ? c.wet : c.dry, id = c.held, n = r.tool === 'cup' ? 1 : r.n;
  d.tipping = true; d.tilt = -60; cfMove(id, ...c.pos, -60, 160);
  document.getElementById('cfBowl').classList.remove('over');
  setTimeout(() => {
    if (cf !== c) return;
    cfPour(c, id, wet); d.tipping = false; d.tilt = 0;
    if (c.count >= n) {   // that was the last scoop: the spoon leaves your hand
      if (c.drag === d) { c.drag = null; c.dragEnd = performance.now(); }
      cfScoopsDone(c, id, wet);
    } else if (c.drag === d) cfMove(id, ...c.pos, 0, 150);
    else cfMove(id, ...CF_HELD, 0, 260);   // you let go while it was pouring
    cfUpdate();
  }, 240);
}
function cfStirAt(dx, dy) {   // where the spoon sits while stirring: anywhere in the food (near and far side), its tip down in it
  const f = cfFood(), ax = f.rx * .66, ay = f.ry * .7, r = Math.hypot(dx / ax, dy / ay);
  if (r > 1) { dx /= r; dy /= r; }
  return [f.cx + dx, f.cy + 12 + dy];
}
const cfStirTilt = (x, y) => { const f = cfFood(); return (x - f.cx) / 4; };   // leans toward the side it is on
function cfStirBy(d) {   // mixing: the two foods turn into one
  const c = cf;
  c.mix = Math.min(1, c.mix + d);
  document.getElementById('cfBowl').classList.remove('on');
  document.getElementById('cfMixIn').setAttribute('opacity', c.mix);
  ['cfDryIn', 'cfWetIn', 'cfKib'].forEach(i => { document.getElementById(i).style.opacity = 1 - c.mix; });
  if (c.mix >= 1 && c.phase === 'stir') {
    c.phase = 'done'; c.stir = null;
    const id = c.held; cfMove(id, ...CF_HELD, 0, 300); c.held = null;
    setTimeout(() => cfMove(id, CF_TOOLS[id].x, CF_HOME_Y, 0, 420), 320);   // up out of the food, then back in its place
    cfUpdate();
    const fx = document.createElementNS('http://www.w3.org/2000/svg', 'g'); document.getElementById('msStage').appendChild(fx);
    const f = cfFood(); starBurst(f.cx, f.cy, fx);
    setTimeout(() => { closeOverlay(document.getElementById('measure')); cf = null; feedPet(); }, 1500);
  }
}
function cfTap(el) {   // taps (and Enter) still work: tap a tool to pick it, the food to scoop, the bowl to tip it in or stir
  if (!cf || cf.anim || cf.drag || performance.now() - (cf.dragEnd || 0) < 350) return;   // (not the click that ends a drag)
  const tool = el.closest('.cf-tool'), src = el.closest('.cf-src'), k = cfSrcKey();
  if (tool && !tool.classList.contains('cf-held')) return cfPickTool(tool.dataset.tool);
  if (k && !cf.loaded && src && src.id === (k === 'dry' ? 'cfDry' : 'cfWet')) return cfDip();
  if (k && cf.loaded && src && src.id === 'cfBowl') return cfTip();
  if ((src && src.id === 'cfBowl' || tool) && cf.phase === 'stir' && performance.now() - (cf.tapped || 0) > 700) cfStirTap();
}
function cfStirTap() {   // a tap on the bowl stirs a little
  if (!cf || !cf.held) return;
  cf.tapped = performance.now();
  const id = cf.held, f = cfFood();   // one full circle around the food
  [0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6].forEach(i => setTimeout(() => {
    if (!cf || !cf.held) return;
    const a = i * Math.PI / 3, [x, y] = i === 6 ? cfStirAt(0, 0) : cfStirAt(Math.cos(a) * f.rx, Math.sin(a) * f.ry);
    cfMove(id, x, y, i === 6 ? 0 : cfStirTilt(x, y), 110);
  }, i * 110));
  cfStirBy(.25);
}
stage.addEventListener('click', e => { if (cf) cfTap(e.target); });
stage.addEventListener('keydown', e => { if (cf && (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ')) { e.preventDefault(); cfTap(e.target); } });
const cfIn = (b, p) => p.x >= b[0] && p.x <= b[0] + b[2] && p.y >= b[1] && p.y <= b[1] + b[3];
const cfBowlBox = () => { const B = CF_BOWL; return [B.x - 20, B.y - 40, B.w + 40, cfFood().bh + 60]; };
function cfDropBox() {   // where the tool in your hand can be let go: the food when it's empty, the bowl when it's full
  const k = cfSrcKey(); return !k ? null : cf.loaded ? cfBowlBox() : CF_SRC[k].box;
}
stage.addEventListener('pointerdown', e => {
  if (!cf || cf.anim) return;
  if (cf.phase === 'stir') {   // stirring: hold the spoon (or the bowl) and move it around in the food
    if (!e.target.closest('#cfBowl, .cf-tool.cf-held')) return;
    e.preventDefault(); cf.stir = msPoint(e); cf.moved = false; stage.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
    return cfUpdate();
  }
  const pick = e.target.closest('.cf-tool:not(.cf-held)');
  if (pick && (cf.phase === 'pickDry' || cf.phase === 'pickWet')) {   // drag a spoon from its place straight to the food
    const id = pick.dataset.tool, r = cf.phase === 'pickDry' ? cf.dry : cf.wet;
    e.preventDefault(); const p = msPoint(e), home = [CF_TOOLS[id].x, CF_HOME_Y];
    if (id !== r.tool) {   // the wrong spoon: you can pick it up, but it shakes and goes back when you let go
      cf.wrongDrag = { id, off: [home[0] - p.x, home[1] - p.y], moved: false };
      stage.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); return;
    }
    clearTimeout(cf.stuck); cf.wrong = 0;
    cf.held = id; cf.count = 0; cf.hint = null; cf.loaded = false; cf.phase = cf.phase === 'pickDry' ? 'scoopDry' : 'scoopWet'; cf.pos = home;
    cf.drag = { off: [home[0] - p.x, home[1] - p.y], from: home, moved: false };
    stage.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); return cfUpdate();
  }
  const k = cfSrcKey(), src = k && e.target.closest(k === 'dry' ? '#cfDry' : '#cfWet');
  if (!k || !(e.target.closest('.cf-tool.cf-held') || (cf.loaded && src))) return;   // dragging the tool to the food, then to the bowl (a full one can be picked up from its food too)
  e.preventDefault(); const p = msPoint(e), onTool = !!e.target.closest('.cf-tool.cf-held');
  cf.drag = { off: onTool ? [cf.pos[0] - p.x, cf.pos[1] - p.y] : [0, -30], from: cf.pos.slice(), moved: false };   // picked up from the food: it jumps to your finger
  stage.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); cfUpdate();
});
stage.addEventListener('pointermove', e => {
  if (!cf) return;
  if (cf.wrongDrag) {
    const p = msPoint(e), d = cf.wrongDrag, x = p.x + d.off[0], y = p.y + d.off[1], t = CF_TOOLS[d.id];
    if (Math.hypot(x - t.x, y - CF_HOME_Y) > 8) d.moved = true;
    cfMove(d.id, x, y, 0, 0); return;
  }
  if (cf.drag) {
    const p = msPoint(e), d = cf.drag, x = p.x + d.off[0], y = p.y + d.off[1]; d.p = p;
    if (Math.hypot(x - d.from[0], y - d.from[1]) > 8) d.moved = true;
    cfMove(cf.held, x, y, d.tilt || 0, 0);
    if (d.tipping) return;   // pouring: it follows your finger, still tipped
    const box = cfDropBox(), over = box && (cfIn(box, { x, y }) || cfIn(box, p));   // the tool or your finger
    if (over && cf.loaded && d.moved) return cfTipHeld(d);   // a full spoon over the bowl: it pours right away, and stays in your hand
    if (over && !cf.loaded) {   // touching the food: it fills right away, no need to let go, and a little scoop tilt
      const k = cfSrcKey(); cf.loaded = true; d.filled = true; d.tilt = 22;
      cfLoad(cf.held, k === 'wet', true);
      document.getElementById(k === 'dry' ? 'cfDry' : 'cfWet').classList.remove('over');
      setTimeout(() => { if (cf && cf.drag === d) { d.tilt = 0; cfMove(cf.held, ...cf.pos, 0, 150); } }, 220);
      return cfUpdate();
    }
    document.getElementById(cf.loaded ? 'cfBowl' : cfSrcKey() === 'dry' ? 'cfDry' : 'cfWet').classList.toggle('over', !!over);
    return;
  }
  if (!cf.stir) return;
  const p = msPoint(e), d = Math.hypot(p.x - cf.stir.x, p.y - cf.stir.y);
  if (d > 6) cf.moved = true;
  const f = cfFood(), [x, y] = cfStirAt(p.x - f.cx, p.y - f.cy);
  cfMove(cf.held, x, y, cfStirTilt(x, y), 0);
  cf.stir = p;
  cfStirBy(d / 1400);
});
const cfStirEnd = () => {
  if (cf && cf.wrongDrag) {   // the wrong spoon: back to its place with a shake (a tap on it is handled by cfTap)
    const d = cf.wrongDrag; cf.wrongDrag = null;
    if (d.moved) { cf.dragEnd = performance.now(); cfMove(d.id, CF_TOOLS[d.id].x, CF_HOME_Y, 0, 250); setTimeout(() => cf && cfPickTool(d.id), 260); }
    return;
  }
  if (cf && cf.drag) {
    const d = cf.drag, box = cfDropBox(), ok = !d.tipping && d.moved && box && (cfIn(box, { x: cf.pos[0], y: cf.pos[1] }) || (d.p && cfIn(box, d.p)));
    cf.drag = null; cf.dragEnd = performance.now(); document.querySelectorAll('.cf-src.over').forEach(g => g.classList.remove('over'));
    if (d.tipping) return;   // let go while pouring: cfTipHeld puts it down
    if (ok) return cf.loaded ? cfTip() : cfDip();
    cfMove(cf.held, ...(cf.loaded ? CF_SRC[cfSrcKey()].rest : d.filled ? CF_HELD : d.from), 0, 250);   // not there yet: full, it waits by the food; empty, it waits in the middle
    return cfUpdate();
  }
  if (cf && cf.stir) { cf.stir = null; if (!cf.moved) return cfStirTap(); if (cf.held) cfMove(cf.held, ...cfStirAt(0, 0), 0, 250); cf.tapped = performance.now(); }
};
stage.addEventListener('pointerup', cfStirEnd);
stage.addEventListener('pointercancel', cfStirEnd);

// ---------- Play (cats): drag the feather wand to the cat tree, and the cat jumps up after it ----------
const TREE_BED = { cx: 884, bottom: 462, k: .55 };   // the bed at the top of the cat tree (farther back, so smaller)
const TREE_FRONT = 'M804 444 Q884 470 964 444 L958 484 Q884 498 810 484 Z';   // the bed's front edge, drawn over the cat's paws
const WAND = { x: 430, y: 945 };                     // where the wand's handle lies on the rug
const FEATHER = [250, -70];                          // the feather, from the handle
function wandSvg() {
  const [fx, fy] = FEATHER;
  return `<g id="wand" class="wand drag" role="button" tabindex="0" aria-label="Feather wand, drag it to the cat tree">
    ${ringSvg(WAND.x - 30, WAND.y - 190, 330, 220)}
    <rect x="${WAND.x - 30}" y="${WAND.y - 190}" width="330" height="220" fill="rgba(255,255,255,0)"/>
    <g id="wandMove"><g transform="translate(${WAND.x} ${WAND.y})">
      <path d="M0 0 L190 -110" stroke="#c98a5a" stroke-width="13" stroke-linecap="round"/>
      <path d="M6 -6 L150 -88" stroke="#e2b07e" stroke-width="4" stroke-linecap="round"/>
      <path d="M190 -110 Q236 -126 ${fx} ${fy}" stroke="#8a7a9c" stroke-width="3" fill="none"/>
      <g class="feather" style="transform-origin: ${fx}px ${fy}px">
        <path d="M${fx} ${fy} C ${fx - 22} ${fy + 20}, ${fx - 18} ${fy + 58}, ${fx - 4} ${fy + 78} C ${fx + 12} ${fy + 56}, ${fx + 14} ${fy + 22}, ${fx} ${fy} Z" fill="#e0457b"/>
        <path d="M${fx} ${fy} C ${fx + 8} ${fy + 22}, ${fx + 30} ${fy + 46}, ${fx + 42} ${fy + 58} C ${fx + 42} ${fy + 34}, ${fx + 24} ${fy + 10}, ${fx} ${fy} Z" fill="#a77bd1"/>
        <path d="M${fx - 1} ${fy + 4} L${fx - 4} ${fy + 66}" stroke="#f7b6cc" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round"/>
        <circle cx="${fx}" cy="${fy}" r="7" fill="#ffd34d"/></g></g></g></g>`;
}
function playTime() {   // the wand lies on the rug, and the cat tree lights up
  document.getElementById('petLayer').insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', wandSvg());
  guide(`Drag the feather wand to the cat tree.`);
}
function wandMove(dx, dy, ms_) {
  const g = document.getElementById('wandMove'); if (!g) return;
  g.style.transition = ms_ ? `transform ${ms_}ms ease-in-out` : 'none';
  g.style.transform = `translate(${dx}px, ${dy}px)`;
}
let wd = null;
room.addEventListener('pointerdown', e => {
  const g = e.target.closest('.wand.drag');
  if (!g || busy) return;
  wd = { start: svgPoint(e), dx: 0, dy: 0 }; g.classList.add('held');
  room.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
});
const TREE_PAD = 70;   // the cat tree notices the wand a little outside its glow, so it is easy to reach
function wandAtTree(d) {   // your finger or the feather is on (or near) the cat tree
  const t = TARGETS.tree, near = (x, y) => x > t.x - TREE_PAD && x < t.x + t.w + TREE_PAD && y > t.y - TREE_PAD && y < t.y + t.h + TREE_PAD;
  return near(d.p.x, d.p.y) || near(WAND.x + FEATHER[0] + d.dx, WAND.y + FEATHER[1] + d.dy);
}
room.addEventListener('pointermove', e => {
  if (!wd) return;
  const p = svgPoint(e); wd.dx = p.x - wd.start.x; wd.dy = p.y - wd.start.y; wd.p = p;
  wandMove(wd.dx, wd.dy, 0);
  if (wd.playing) return;
  if (wandAtTree(wd)) { wd.playing = true; dropHint(null); playAtTree(wd.dx, wd.dy, true); }   // it reached the tree: the cat goes after it while you hold it
  else dropHint(null);
});
room.addEventListener('pointerup', () => {
  if (!wd) return;
  const d = wd; wd = null; dropHint(null);
  const g = document.getElementById('wand'); if (g) g.classList.remove('held');
  if (d.playing) return wandSnap();   // let go while the cat plays: the wand hangs above the bed and keeps wiggling
  if (d.p && wandAtTree(d)) playAtTree(d.dx, d.dy);
  else wandMove(0, 0, 400);   // not on the tree: back on the rug
});
function wandSnapTo() { return [TREE_BED.cx - (WAND.x + FEATHER[0]) + (pet.paw === 'left' ? -62 : 46), TREE_BED.bottom - 217 - (WAND.y + FEATHER[1])]; }   // just above the paw she lifts
function wandSnap() { const w = document.getElementById('wand'); if (!w) return; wandMove(...wandSnapTo(), 450); setTimeout(() => w.classList.add('wiggle'), 400); }
function petImg(pose, cx, bottom, h) {   // a pose on the rug, the same size as sitting
  const img = document.querySelector('.petg image'), b = poseBox(pose, cx, bottom, h);
  img.setAttribute('href', ART[POSES[pose].art(pet)]); img.setAttribute('x', b.x); img.setAttribute('y', b.y); img.setAttribute('width', b.w); img.setAttribute('height', b.h);
  return img;
}
function jumpPet(from, to, ms_, done) {   // a cat leap: crouch and wiggle, spring up along an arc (stretched out), land with a little squash
  const g = document.querySelector('.petg'), t0 = performance.now(), crouch = 380, land = 220, lean = to[0] < from[0] ? -1 : 1;
  g.style.transition = 'none'; g.style.transformOrigin = `${RUG.cx}px ${RUG.bottom}px`;
  const put = (x, y, s, sx, sy, rot) => { g.style.transform = `translate(${x}px, ${y}px) scale(${s * sx}, ${s * sy}) rotate(${rot}deg)`; };
  const step = t => {
    const e = t - t0;
    if (e < crouch) {   // down low, with a little side to side wiggle, leaning toward where it will jump
      const k = e / crouch, low = Math.sin(Math.min(1, k * 1.6) * Math.PI / 2);
      put(from[0] + Math.sin(k * Math.PI * 4) * 4, from[1], from[2], 1 + .06 * low, 1 - .1 * low, lean * 3 * low);
    } else if (e < crouch + ms_) {   // in the air: long and stretched in the middle, tipped the way it is going
      const k = (e - crouch) / ms_, up = 4 * 170 * k * (1 - k), str = Math.sin(k * Math.PI);
      const x = from[0] + (to[0] - from[0]) * k, y = from[1] + (to[1] - from[1]) * k - up, sc = from[2] + (to[2] - from[2]) * k;
      put(x, y, sc, 1 - .05 * str, 1 + .09 * str, lean * 8 * (1 - 2 * k));
    } else if (e < crouch + ms_ + land) {   // landing: squash, then back to normal
      const k = (e - crouch - ms_) / land, sq = Math.sin(k * Math.PI);
      put(to[0], to[1], to[2], 1 + .07 * sq, 1 - .1 * sq, 0);
    } else { put(to[0], to[1], to[2], 1, 1, 0); return done(); }
    requestAnimationFrame(step);
  };
  requestAnimationFrame(step);
}
function swats(n, done) {   // batting at the feather: the paw goes up (play picture) and down (sitting), and the feather flicks away each time
  const fea = document.querySelector('#wand .feather');
  let i = 0;
  const one = () => {
    if (i++ >= n) { petImg('play', RUG.cx, RUG.bottom, rugH()); return done(); }
    petImg('play', RUG.cx, RUG.bottom, rugH());
    if (fea) { fea.style.translate = `${i % 2 ? 14 : -10}px -16px`; setTimeout(() => { fea.style.translate = '0 0'; }, 170); }
    setTimeout(() => { petImg(pet.playDown ? 'playDown' : 'sit', RUG.cx, RUG.bottom, rugH()); setTimeout(one, 230 + (i % 2) * 90); }, 260);
  };
  one();
}
function playAtTree(fromDx, fromDy, held) {   // held: you are still holding the wand, so it stays in your hand until you let go
  if (busy || !TASKS[task] || TASKS[task].icon !== 'feather') return;
  busy = true;
  const wand = document.getElementById('wand');
  wand.classList.remove('drag');
  room.querySelector('[data-t="tree"]').classList.remove('on');
  guideEl.classList.add('hide');
  // the wand glides up so the feather dangles just above the bed, and wiggles
  if (!held) { wandMove(fromDx, fromDy, 0); requestAnimationFrame(wandSnap); }
  const bed = [TREE_BED.cx - RUG.cx, TREE_BED.bottom - RUG.bottom, TREE_BED.k];
  setTimeout(() => {   // up the tree after it, paws tucked in (the sitting picture)
    petImg('sit', RUG.cx, RUG.bottom, rugH());
    jumpPet([0, 0, 1], bed, 620, () => {
      document.getElementById('treeFront').style.opacity = 1;
      swats(5, () => setTimeout(() => {   // back down, happy
        document.getElementById('treeFront').style.opacity = 0;
        petImg('sit', RUG.cx, RUG.bottom, rugH());
        wand.classList.remove('wiggle'); wand.style.transition = 'opacity .4s'; wand.style.opacity = 0;
        jumpPet(bed, [0, 0, 1], 620, playDone);
      }, 350));
    });
  }, 700);
}
function playDone() {
  const img = petImg('done', RUG.cx, RUG.bottom, rugH());
  const fx = document.createElementNS('http://www.w3.org/2000/svg', 'g'); room.appendChild(fx);
  starBurst(RUG.cx, RUG.bottom - rugH() * .6, fx); setTimeout(() => fx.remove(), 1200);
  const w = document.getElementById('wand'); if (w) w.remove();
  busy = false; setTask(task + 1);
  document.getElementById('adTitle').textContent = 'Thanks for your help!';
  const paid = pet.name; setTimeout(() => payMe(10, `Helped ${paid} at the Vet Clinic`), 900);   // the All Done card says thanks and pays you
}

// ---------- Toys from the backpack: open the backpack and drag a toy to the pet (or tap it) to give it; it is used up ----------
const TOY_FOR = { catToy: 'cat', dogToy: 'dog' };
const toyBeep = (n, t) => { if (window.tbBeep) tbBeep(n, t); };
const TOY_SAD = [[330, 0, .15], [262, .12, .2]];
const toyToast = msg => showToast(msg, 3800);   // long enough to read
function closeBag() { const p = document.getElementById('tbPanel'); if (p) p.classList.remove('open'); }
function heartSvg(cx, cy, r, col, delay) {   // one soft heart that pops up, twinkles and fades where it is
  return `<path class="toy-heart" style="animation-delay:${delay}ms" fill="${col}"
    d="M${cx} ${cy + r * .9} C${cx - r * 1.6} ${cy - r * .1} ${cx - r * .9} ${cy - r * 1.3} ${cx} ${cy - r * .45} C${cx + r * .9} ${cy - r * 1.3} ${cx + r * 1.6} ${cy - r * .1} ${cx} ${cy + r * .9} Z"/>`;
}
function heartsAround(cx, cy, rx, ry) {   // hearts and little sparkles all around and above the pet, a few at a time
  const COLS = ['#ff8fb1', '#f7a8c4', '#e0457b'];
  let s = '';
  for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
    const a = Math.PI * (1.08 + .84 * (i / 9)) + (i % 2 ? .08 : -.08), d = .78 + (i * 37 % 10) / 45;   // from the left side, over the top, to the right side
    const x = cx + Math.cos(a) * rx * d, y = cy + Math.sin(a) * ry * d, delay = (i * 7 % 10) * 230;
    s += heartSvg(x, y, 20 + (i * 3) % 12, COLS[i % 3], delay);
    const sx = x + (i % 2 ? 34 : -34), sy = y - 18, r = 11 + i % 3 * 3;
    s += `<polygon class="toy-spark" style="animation-delay:${delay + 380}ms" fill="${i % 3 ? '#fff6b0' : '#fff'}" stroke="#ffc94d" stroke-width="2"
      points="${sx},${sy - r} ${sx + r * .3},${sy - r * .3} ${sx + r},${sy} ${sx + r * .3},${sy + r * .3} ${sx},${sy + r} ${sx - r * .3},${sy + r * .3} ${sx - r},${sy} ${sx - r * .3},${sy - r * .3}"/>`;
  }
  return s;
}
const PLAY2_K = 1.1, PLAY2_EACH = { peaches: 1.2, miso: 1.12, jax: 1.1 };   // a few cats are drawn smaller in their playing picture; this keeps their heads the sitting size   // the playing-with-a-toy pictures are all cut at the same scale; this makes them the sitting size on the rug
function play2Box() {   // the crouched playing picture on the rug (it faces left, so the toy goes in front of its paws on the left)
  const im = new Image(); im.src = ART[pet.play2];
  const k = rugH() / 560 * PLAY2_K * (PLAY2_EACH[pet.id] || 1), w = (im.naturalWidth || 500) * k, h = (im.naturalHeight || 400) * k;
  return { x: RUG.cx - w / 2, y: RUG.bottom - h, w, h };
}
function toyCheck(id) {   // says what is wrong, or nothing when this toy can be given now
  const it = (window.tbItems || []).find(t => t.id === id) || { id, name: 'that' };
  if (!pet) return 'Pick a pet first, then give a toy.';
  const forKind = TOY_FOR[it.kind];
  if (!forKind) return `${pet.name} can't play with that.`;
  if (forKind !== kind) return `${it.name} is for ${forKind}s. ${pet.name} is a ${kind}!`;
  const g = document.querySelector('.petg');
  if (busy || drag || wdrag || wd || document.querySelector('.overlay.open:not(#allDone)') || !g || g.dataset.toy) return `Give the toy when ${pet.name} is back on the rug.`;
  return '';
}
// How each cat plays with each toy (Mac's Pet Store pictures): crouched, a front paw on the toy, the toy on the floor at the paw.
// The Cat Couches are for napping: the cat curls up asleep in the couch. The toy then stays on the rug by the pet until a new pet is picked.
const TOY_W = { 'cat-ball-tower': .62, 'cat-couch-blue': 1.35, 'cat-couch-pink': 1.35 };   // couches: big enough for the cat at its own size (Mac, Nov 25, 2026)   // how wide each toy is, as parts of the pet's height (others .3)
const TOY_REST = { cx: 610, bottom: 995 };   // where a Cat Couch waits on the floor, left of the rug, clear of the food bowl (a second couch goes on the right)
// where toys wait after playing: on the light middle of the rug, close to the pet; every toy given stays (up to 6, then the oldest goes).
// x is how far from the pet's middle (left), bottom is where the toy sits. The first two are the closest; dogs use the mirror image (they play on the right).
const TOY_SPOTS = [{ dx: -205, bottom: 975 }, { dx: 210, bottom: 975 }, { dx: -300, bottom: 940 }, { dx: 300, bottom: 940 }, { dx: -270, bottom: 885 }, { dx: 270, bottom: 885 }];
function toySpot(couch) {   // the first free resting spot (the oldest toy makes room when all are taken)
  const rest = [...document.querySelectorAll('#petLayer .toy-rest:not(.gone)')].filter(t => (t.dataset.couch === '1') === couch);
  const n = couch ? 2 : TOY_SPOTS.length, used = rest.map(t => +t.dataset.spot);
  let i = [...Array(n).keys()].find(k => !used.includes(k));
  if (i === undefined) { const old = rest[0]; i = +old.dataset.spot; old.classList.add('gone'); setTimeout(() => old.remove(), 500); }
  if (couch) return Object.assign({ i }, i ? { cx: 2 * RUG.cx - TOY_REST.cx, bottom: TOY_REST.bottom } : TOY_REST);
  const s = TOY_SPOTS[i], k = rugH() / RUG.h;   // a little closer in for smaller pets
  return { i, cx: RUG.cx + s.dx * (kind === 'dog' ? -1 : 1) * (.55 + .45 * k), bottom: s.bottom };
}
const PAW_TIP = { finley: .1 };   // where the front paw is in the playing picture, as part of its width (most are right at the left edge)
const isCouch = id => /^cat-couch/.test(id);
function toySize(id) {
  const im = new Image(); im.src = tbItemPic(id);
  const w = rugH() * (TOY_W[id] || .3), h = w * ((im.naturalHeight / im.naturalWidth) || .8);
  return { w, h };
}
function toySound(id) {
  if (id === 'cat-bell-ball') return toyBeep([[1568, 0, .12], [2093, .1, .12], [1568, .2, .12], [2093, .3, .12], [1760, .42, .25]], 'sine');   // jingle
  if (id === 'cat-zoom-ball') return toyBeep(Array.from({ length: 9 }, (_, i) => [300 + i * 110, i * .035, .06]), 'sine');   // zoop
  if (isCouch(id)) return toyBeep([[523, 0, .3], [392, .3, .45], [330, .9, .5]], 'sine');   // a soft, sleepy sigh
  toyBeep([[784, 0, .12], [988, .12, .12], [1319, .24, .3]]);
}
function giveToy(id) {
  const no = toyCheck(id);
  if (no) { toyBeep(TOY_SAD, 'sine'); return toyToast(no); }
  const it = tbItems.find(t => t.id === id);
  if (!window.tbRemoveItem || !tbRemoveItem(id)) return;   // used up
  const ad = document.getElementById('allDone'), endCard = ad.classList.contains('open');
  closeBag(); if (endCard) closeOverlay(ad);
  const g = document.querySelector('.petg'), img = g.querySelector('image'), layer = document.getElementById('petLayer');
  g.dataset.toy = id;
  const keep = ['href', 'x', 'y', 'width', 'height'].map(a => [a, img.getAttribute(a)]);
  const couch = isCouch(id), sz = toySize(id), cat = kind === 'cat' && ART[pet.play2] && !couch, pb = cat ? play2Box() : null, spot = toySpot(couch);
  // where it plays: cats with a toy, at the front paw; a couch, at the waiting spot; dogs, by their front paws
  const at = cat ? { cx: pb.x + pb.w * (PAW_TIP[pet.id] || .02) + sz.w * .12, bottom: RUG.bottom + 4 }
    : couch ? spot : { cx: RUG.cx + rugH() * .38, bottom: RUG.bottom };
  const ns = 'http://www.w3.org/2000/svg', toy = document.createElementNS(ns, 'g');
  toy.setAttribute('class', 'toy-rest'); toy.dataset.spot = spot.i; toy.dataset.couch = couch ? '1' : '0'; toy.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
  toy.innerHTML = `<g class="toy-slide"><image class="toy-given${couch ? ' soft' : ''}" href="${tbItemPic(id)}" x="${at.cx - sz.w / 2}" y="${at.bottom - sz.h}" width="${sz.w}" height="${sz.h}"/></g>`;
  layer.insertBefore(toy, g);   // on the floor, behind the pet (the paw goes over it)
  const fx = document.createElementNS(ns, 'g'); fx.style.pointerEvents = 'none'; layer.appendChild(fx);
  toyBeep([[523, 0, .1], [659, .08, .1], [784, .16, .1], [1047, .26, .3]]);
  let nap = null;
  setTimeout(() => {
    if (couch && ART[pet.id + 'Sleep']) {   // the cat curls up asleep in the couch, the couch's front edge over it
      const w = sz.w * .66, h = w * 400 / 498, x = at.cx - w / 2, y = at.bottom - sz.h * .27 - h, top = at.bottom - sz.h * .3;
      nap = document.createElementNS(ns, 'g'); nap.setAttribute('class', 'toy-nap');
      nap.innerHTML = `<image href="${ART[pet.id + 'Sleep']}" x="${x}" y="${y}" width="${w}" height="${h}"/>
        <clipPath id="couchFront"><rect x="${at.cx - sz.w / 2}" y="${top}" width="${sz.w}" height="${sz.h}"/></clipPath>
        <image href="${tbItemPic(id)}" x="${at.cx - sz.w / 2}" y="${at.bottom - sz.h}" width="${sz.w}" height="${sz.h}" clip-path="url(#couchFront)"/>`;
      toy.appendChild(nap); g.classList.add('toy-away');
    } else if (cat) {   // crouched, a paw on the toy, little taps
      img.setAttribute('href', ART[pet.play2]); img.setAttribute('x', pb.x); img.setAttribute('y', pb.y); img.setAttribute('width', pb.w); img.setAttribute('height', pb.h);
      img.classList.add('toy-tap'); toy.querySelector('.toy-given').classList.add('wiggle');
    } else { img.classList.add('toy-hop'); toy.querySelector('.toy-given').classList.add('wiggle'); }
    const hb = couch ? { cx: at.cx, cy: at.bottom - sz.h * .45, rx: sz.w * .62, ry: sz.h * .75 }   // around the couch and the cat asleep in it
      : { cx: RUG.cx, cy: RUG.bottom - rugH() * .5, rx: rugH() * .62, ry: rugH() * .62 };
    fx.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', heartsAround(hb.cx, hb.cy, hb.rx, hb.ry));
    toyToast(couch ? `${pet.name} loves the new bed! Time for a cat nap.` : `${pet.name} loves the ${it.name}!`);
    toySound(id);
  }, 450);
  setTimeout(() => {   // back the way it was, and the toy goes to wait on the rug by the pet
    const now = document.querySelector('.petg image');
    if (now) { now.classList.remove('toy-tap', 'toy-hop'); keep.forEach(([a, v]) => now.setAttribute(a, v)); }
    g.classList.remove('toy-away'); if (nap) nap.remove();
    const t = toy.querySelector('.toy-given'); if (t) { t.classList.remove('wiggle'); t.classList.add('still'); }
    toy.querySelector('.toy-slide').style.transform = `translate(${spot.cx - at.cx}px, ${spot.bottom - at.bottom}px)`;   // to its own spot by the pet
    delete g.dataset.toy;
    if (endCard && pet) openOverlay(ad);
  }, couch ? 6200 : 4200);   // a little longer to enjoy it (Mac, Nov 25, 2026)
  setTimeout(() => fx.remove(), couch ? 6600 : 5400);
}
const bagPanel = document.getElementById('tbPanel');
const slotToy = slot => { const id = tbBag()[[...slot.parentNode.children].indexOf(slot)]; return id && ((window.tbItems || []).find(x => x.id === id) || {}).play ? null : id; };   // games are not toys: the toolbar asks Play now
function emptyBag() { toyBeep(TOY_SAD, 'sine'); toyToast('Your backpack is empty. Toys are at the Department Store.'); }
let toyDrag = null, toyDragged = false;
const overPet = (x, y) => { const g = document.querySelector('.petg'); if (!g) return false; const r = g.getBoundingClientRect(); return x > r.left && x < r.right && y > r.top && y < r.bottom; };
bagPanel?.addEventListener('pointerdown', e => {   // pick a toy up out of the backpack
  const slot = e.target.closest('.tb-slot'), id = slot && slotToy(slot);
  if (!id) return;
  const r = slot.getBoundingClientRect();
  toyDrag = { id, x0: e.clientX, y0: e.clientY, home: [r.left + r.width / 2, r.top + r.height / 2], ghost: null };
});
document.addEventListener('pointermove', e => {
  const d = toyDrag; if (!d) return;
  if (!d.ghost) {
    if (Math.hypot(e.clientX - d.x0, e.clientY - d.y0) < 8) return;
    d.ghost = Object.assign(document.createElement('img'), { className: 'toy-ghost', src: tbItemPic(d.id), alt: '' });
    document.body.appendChild(d.ghost); toyBeep([[523, 0, .08], [659, .06, .1]]);
  }
  d.ghost.style.left = e.clientX + 'px'; d.ghost.style.top = e.clientY + 'px';
  const g = document.querySelector('.petg'); if (g) g.classList.toggle('toy-over', overPet(e.clientX, e.clientY));
});
function toyDrop(e) {
  const d = toyDrag; toyDrag = null; if (!d || !d.ghost) return;
  toyDragged = true; setTimeout(() => { toyDragged = false; }, 0);   // a drag is not also a tap
  const g = document.querySelector('.petg'); if (g) g.classList.remove('toy-over');
  if (e.type === 'pointerup' && overPet(e.clientX, e.clientY)) { d.ghost.remove(); return giveToy(d.id); }
  d.ghost.classList.add('back'); d.ghost.style.left = d.home[0] + 'px'; d.ghost.style.top = d.home[1] + 'px';   // let go somewhere else: back into the backpack
  setTimeout(() => d.ghost.remove(), 320);
}
document.addEventListener('pointerup', toyDrop);
document.addEventListener('pointercancel', toyDrop);
bagPanel?.addEventListener('click', e => {   // a tap on a toy gives it too
  if (toyDragged) return;
  const slot = e.target.closest('.tb-slot'); if (!slot) return;
  const id = slotToy(slot);
  const it = id && (window.tbItems || []).find(x => x.id === id);
  if (it && pet && it.kind === kind + 'Toy' && document.querySelector('.petg')) {   // tapping only says how: drag it out (activity-rules.md, Mac, Nov 26, 2026)
    e.tbHandled = true; slot.classList.remove('tb-wiggle'); void slot.offsetWidth; toyToast(`Drag the ${it.name} to ${pet.name}.`); return;
  }
  if (!tbBag().length) emptyBag();
});
document.getElementById('tbBag')?.addEventListener('click', () => {   // opening the backpack with a pet out: say what to do
  setTimeout(() => {
    if (!bagPanel.classList.contains('open')) return;
    if (!tbBag().length) return toyToast('Your backpack is empty. Toys are at the Department Store.');
    if (pet && tbBag().some(t => ((window.tbItems || []).find(x => x.id === t) || {}).kind === kind + 'Toy')) toyToast(`Drag a toy to ${pet.name}.`);
  }, 0);
});

const COIN = '<svg viewBox="0 0 64 36"><rect x="1" y="3" width="62" height="32" rx="5" fill="#468a5e"/><rect x="1" y="1" width="62" height="32" rx="5" fill="#8fd1a4"/>' +   // a dollar bill (Mac earns dollars, Nov 25, 2026)
  '<rect x="5" y="5" width="54" height="24" rx="3" fill="none" stroke="#5fae7a" stroke-width="2.4"/><ellipse cx="32" cy="17" rx="10" ry="10" fill="#e6f8ee"/>' +
  '<circle cx="12" cy="17" r="3" fill="#5fae7a"/><circle cx="52" cy="17" r="3" fill="#5fae7a"/>' +
  '<text x="32" y="23.5" text-anchor="middle" font-family="Verdana, Geneva, sans-serif" font-size="17" font-weight="bold" fill="#3e6e4b">$</text></svg>';

  // the vet from the career card in town
function payMe(amount, words) {   // the All Done card: the vet says thanks, the dollar bills fly from it into your money, then its buttons show
  const ad = document.getElementById('allDone'), m = document.getElementById('adMoney');
  document.getElementById('adDoll').src = CAREER_DOLL;
  m.innerHTML = `${COIN}<span>+$${amount}</span>`;
  ad.classList.remove('paid'); openOverlay(ad);
  toyBeep([[523, 0, .1], [659, .1, .1], [784, .2, .1], [1047, .3, .35]]);
  setTimeout(() => {
    const to = document.getElementById('tbCard'), c = m.querySelector('svg').getBoundingClientRect();
    const t = to ? to.getBoundingClientRect() : { left: innerWidth - 80, top: innerHeight - 60, width: 60, height: 40 };
    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) setTimeout(() => {   // the bills fly into your money
      const coin = document.createElement('div'); coin.className = 'pay-coin'; coin.innerHTML = COIN;
      coin.style.left = (c.left + c.width / 2 + i * 6) + 'px'; coin.style.top = (c.top + c.height / 2) + 'px';
      document.body.appendChild(coin); void coin.offsetWidth;
      coin.style.left = (t.left + t.width / 2) + 'px'; coin.style.top = (t.top + t.height / 2) + 'px'; coin.style.transform = 'scale(.6) rotate(200deg)';
      setTimeout(() => { coin.remove(); toyBeep([[1319 + i * 60, 0, .08]]); }, 760);
    }, i * 110);
    setTimeout(() => { if (window.tbEarn) tbEarn(amount, words); ad.classList.add('paid'); }, 6 * 110 + 760);   // all the money is in: now the buttons
  }, 1400);
}
document.getElementById('adTown').addEventListener('click', () => document.querySelector('header .backtown').click());

// ---------- Taps and keys ----------
room.addEventListener('click', e => { const g = e.target.closest('.tgt'); if (g) useTarget(g); });
room.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
  if (e.key !== 'Enter' && e.key !== ' ') return;
  const g = e.target.closest('.tgt'), p = e.target.closest('.petg.drag');
  if (g || p) { e.preventDefault(); g ? useTarget(g) : ['bubbles', 'brush'].includes(TASKS[task].icon) ? goToBath() : weigh(); }
});
document.addEventListener('keydown', e => { if (e.key === 'Escape' && picker.classList.contains('open')) closePicker(); });
// the yellow task in the task bar starts that task, the same as tapping its spot in the room
document.getElementById('tasks').addEventListener('click', e => {
  const t = TASKS[task];
  if (!e.target.closest('.task.cur') || !t || busy) return;
  if (t.icon === 'scale') return weigh();
  if (t.icon === 'bubbles' || t.icon === 'brush') return goToBath();
  const id = t.icon === 'bowl' && fed && !watered ? 'water' : t.targets[0];
  useTarget(room.querySelector(`[data-t="${id}"]`));
});
// make the task bar and room as big as the screen allows (the room keeps its shape)
const frame = document.getElementById('frame');
// on short screens (a phone on its side) Back to town moves into the task bar's left end, so the room can be taller
const short = matchMedia('(max-height: 600px)'), backBtn = document.querySelector('header .backtown');
function placeBack() { if (short.matches) document.querySelector('.taskbar').prepend(backBtn); else document.querySelector('header').prepend(backBtn); fitFrame(); }
short.addEventListener('change', placeBack);
// the building's badge in the task bar: the Vet Clinic logo (mint cross with a cat and a dog) on a raised white badge
document.getElementById('bldgLogo').innerHTML = `<img src="${ART.vetLogo}" alt="Vet Clinic logo">`;
function fitFrame() {
  const g = document.getElementById('game'), cs = getComputedStyle(g), hd = document.querySelector('header');
  const w = g.clientWidth - parseFloat(cs.paddingLeft) - parseFloat(cs.paddingRight);
  const top = short.matches ? 0 : hd.offsetHeight + 8;
  const h = g.clientHeight - parseFloat(cs.paddingTop) - parseFloat(cs.paddingBottom) - top - document.querySelector('.taskbar').offsetHeight;
  frame.style.width = Math.max(280, Math.min(w, h * 1984 / 1116)) + 'px';
}
addEventListener('resize', fitFrame); placeBack();
new ResizeObserver(fitFrame).observe(document.querySelector('.taskbar'));   // the task bar grows a little when the tasks come in

drawRoom();
setTask(0, true);
// coming from the town's crates: open that picker right away
{ const k = location.hash.match(/^#(dog|cat)\b/); if (k) { openPicker(k[1]); history.replaceState(null, '', location.pathname); } }   // only once
